TC-ROOM-011 Baselines are editable until the room's first reading exists, then locked e2eP0 Room 517 exists with baselines 1,250 / 5,280 and no meter_readings row
  1. Admin edits baseline_water 1,250 → 1,251, saves
  2. Reset it to 1,250
  3. Staff saves the room's first reading for 2026-09
  4. Admin reopens /rooms/:id/edit
  5. POST /rooms/:id with a changed baseline anyway
 baseline_water 1,250 → 1,251 → 1,250; then water current 1260, electric current 5350 Steps 1–2 succeed. After step 3 the three baseline inputs render readonly with dashed borders, the card badge reads ล็อกแล้ว and the note reads ห้องนี้มีการจดมิเตอร์แล้ว 1 รอบ จึงแก้ไขเลขเริ่มต้นไม่ได้. Step 5 is refused server-side with 422 BASELINE_LOCKED, ห้องนี้มีการจดมิเตอร์แล้ว แก้ไขเลขมิเตอร์เริ่มต้นไม่ได้ — a readonly input is never the guard. FR-41, D-06, D-40

4.2 Rooms — Occupied/Vacant status, deactivation and deletion

Room status is the switch that decides whether a room is billed at all (D-07). It is derived, never entered, and no screen in the product offers a control for it. These ten cases prove that the only two writers of rooms.status are the tenant transactions, and that a room carrying history can be retired — and put back — but never erased.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-ROOM-012 Room status cannot be set by hand from any surface APIP0 Admin session; room 203 is Vacant, room 41 (205) is Occupied
  1. Inspect /rooms/new and /rooms/41/edit for a status control
  2. POST /rooms/<203> including status=Occupied
  3. POST /rooms/41 including status=Vacant
 status=Occupied · status=Vacant injected into an otherwise valid body Neither form renders a status input; both render the sentence สถานะห้องกำหนดจากผู้เช่าโดยอัตโนมัติ. Both posts return 303 and the injected field is ignored, not merged: room 203 is still Vacant and room 41 is still Occupied. No audit_log row records a status change. FR-04, D-07, D-47
TC-ROOM-013 Creating an Active tenant flips the room to Occupied in the same transaction e2eP0 Admin session; room 208 is Vacant with no Active tenant
  1. Open /tenants/new
  2. Select ห้อง 208, fill the form, press บันทึก
  3. Open /rooms/208 and /readings
 ชื่อ-นามสกุล สุชาติ พงษ์ไพบูลย์ · เบอร์โทรศัพท์ 0899990001 · ห้อง 208 · วันที่เข้าอยู่ 2026-09-01 The form states the consequence before the save: สถานะห้อง 208 จะเปลี่ยนเป็น มีผู้เช่า ทันทีที่บันทึก. After saving, rooms.status = 'Occupied', the detail badge reads มีผู้เช่า, the chips read มีผู้เช่า 68 · ว่าง 12, and room 208 now appears in the จดมิเตอร์ counter denominator (68), not in the ห้องว่าง chip. FR-05, D-07, D-10, D-15
TC-ROOM-014 Ending a tenancy flips the room to Vacant in the same transaction e2eP0 Room 207's tenant กิตติศักดิ์ พูนสิน is Active, with both final readings recorded for the current period and that bill Paid — the state TC-ROOM-028 leaves behind. Not room 312: the seed already ships that tenancy completed (Inactive, Paid final bill, plus a new Active tenant from the 1st), and ENG-1's UNIQUE(active_room_id) forbids a second Active tenant on it, so 312 cannot be settled again without unwinding the state TC-ROOM-031 … 033 depend on.
  1. Open the tenant detail
  2. Press สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า
  3. Type a reason of ≥ 10 characters, confirm
  4. Open /rooms/207
 เหตุผล ครบกำหนดสัญญา ผู้เช่าแจ้งย้ายออก rooms.status for 207 becomes Vacant; the badge reads ว่าง and the tenant card becomes the empty state ห้องนี้ยังไม่มีผู้เช่า with เพิ่มผู้เช่า. Chip counts move to มีผู้เช่า 66 · ว่าง 14. Room 207 leaves the จดมิเตอร์ counter and appears under the ห้องว่าง chip. FR-36, D-07, D-08, D-10
TC-ROOM-015 A Vacant room is skipped entirely by the billing run e2eP0 Room 203 is Vacant; a voluntary reading exists for it in 2026-09; 67 rooms are Occupied
  1. Run POST /api/bills/generate for 2026-09
  2. Query bills for room 203
  3. Attempt a single-room generation against room 203
 {"period": "2026-09"}; common rate 35.00 THB/ตร.ม.; area 30.00 ตร.ม. No bills row and no bill_items row exists for room 203 — no bill, no zero bill, no common-area fee of 30.00 × 35.00 = 1,050.00. The run's created count never includes it and it is absent from missing_rooms. The single-room attempt returns 422 ROOM_NOT_BILLABLE, ห้องนี้ไม่มีผู้เช่า จึงไม่ออกบิล. The voluntary reading row still exists and is untouched. FR-16, FR-17, D-07, D-23
TC-ROOM-016 A room no bill references can be deleted outright APIP1 Room 518 created for this test; it has never been billed
  1. Confirm the detail offers ลบห้อง and not ปิดใช้งานห้อง
  2. POST /rooms/<518>/delete
 room 518, floor 5, 30.00 ตร.ม., no bills rows 303 → /rooms; the row is gone from every filter including ปิดใช้งาน; SELECT COUNT(*) FROM rooms WHERE room_code='518' returns 0. Chip totals drop by one. FR-45, D-47
TC-ROOM-017 A room any bill references cannot be deleted APIP0 Room 41 (205) has bill 118 and eleven history bills
  1. Open /rooms/41
  2. POST /rooms/41/delete
  3. Re-open /bills/118
 room id 41, bill 118 total 1,790.00 The detail offers ปิดใช้งานห้อง and no ลบห้อง. The post returns 409 ROOM_HAS_BILLS, ห้องนี้มีบิลแล้ว ลบไม่ได้ กรุณาใช้ปิดใช้งานห้อง. The room row and all twelve bills are intact; bill 118 still renders 180.00 + 560.00 + 1,050.00 = 1,790.00 — the reprint guarantee is not breakable through a delete. FR-45, D-47, D-09
TC-ROOM-018 ปิดใช้งานห้อง — reason gate, audit row and exclusion from every list e2eP1 Admin session; room 108 is Vacant with eleven Paid bills
  1. Open /rooms/108, press ปิดใช้งานห้อง
  2. Type 9 characters, observe the primary button
  3. Extend the reason past 10 characters and confirm
  4. Check /rooms, /readings and the ปิดใช้งาน chip
  5. Run generation for 2026-09
 reason ปิดปรับปรุง (9 chars) then ปิดปรับปรุงห้องน้ำรั่ว The dialog title reads ปิดใช้งานห้อง 108, the body carries ห้องนี้จะไม่ถูกสร้างบิล ไม่ปรากฏในหน้าจดมิเตอร์ และไม่แสดงในรายการห้อง ยกเว้นเมื่อเลือกตัวกรอง ปิดใช้งาน and, in bold, ประวัติบิลและใบเสร็จจะยังคงอยู่. At 9 characters the primary button (labelled ปิดใช้งานห้อง, never ตกลง) stays .is-disabled. On confirm: one audit_log row, action ROOM_DEACTIVATE, reason stored; the detail shows the banner ห้องนี้ถูกปิดใช้งาน เมื่อ 7 ก.ย. 2026 · เหตุผล: ปิดปรับปรุงห้องน้ำรั่ว with เปิดใช้งานอีกครั้ง. The room is absent from ทั้งหมด / มีผู้เช่า / ว่าง, present only under ปิดใช้งาน (count 1), absent from /readings, and produces no bill. Its eleven bills still render. FR-45, FR-46, D-39, D-38, D-47
TC-ROOM-070 เปิดใช้งานอีกครั้ง puts a deactivated room back and audits ROOM_ACTIVATE e2eP1 Admin session; room 108 is is_active = 0 — the state TC-ROOM-018 leaves behind — and its Active tenant and eleven historical bills are untouched.
  1. Open room 108's detail and read the deactivation banner.
  2. Press เปิดใช้งานอีกครั้ง.
  3. Re-read /rooms under each of the four filters and their counts.
  4. Open /readings for the current period.
  5. Run POST /api/bills/generate for the period.
  6. Read audit_log for room 108.
 POST /rooms/:id/activate with _csrf and nothing else — no reason field, because reinstating a room takes nothing away 303 → /rooms/:id with the Thai flash strip; rooms.is_active = 1. The deactivation banner is gone. The room is back in ทั้งหมด and in มีผู้เช่า or ว่าง according to its tenancy, and the ปิดใช้งาน filter count drops to 0. It reappears on /readings and, once both readings exist, the generation run creates its bill again and counts it in created. Exactly one new audit_log row, action ROOM_ACTIVATE, actor = the acting Admin, written on the same connection as the update; the earlier ROOM_DEACTIVATE row and its reason are still there — the history of the round trip is not erased. The eleven historical bills still render 180.00 + 560.00 + 1,050.00 = 1,790.00. FR-45, FR-39 · D-47, D-38, D-07
TC-ROOM-071 Activating an already-active room is a no-op, not a 409 APIP2 Admin session; room 108 is is_active = 1 — the state TC-ROOM-070 leaves behind. Note the current audit_log row count for the room.
  1. POST /rooms/:id/activate a second time.
  2. Issue the same POST twice concurrently, as a double-click does.
  3. Re-read rooms.is_active and audit_log.
 Room 108, _csrf only 303 → /rooms/:id every time — never a 409 and never an error page; the specification makes activation deliberately idempotent so that a double-click on the banner cannot break in front of an examiner, and there is no ROOM_ALREADY_ACTIVE code in the §3.6 catalogue to assert. is_active stays 1. The room's bills, tenancy and readings are untouched. The audit row count is recorded rather than asserted: the specification fixes the response, not whether a no-op writes a second ROOM_ACTIVATE row. If the office ever needs an exact reinstatement count, that becomes a specification question first and an assertion here second. FR-45, FR-48 · D-47, D-38
TC-ROOM-072 Staff cannot deactivate or activate a room APIP0 A staff session. Room 108 active, room 109 deactivated.
  1. Open room 108's detail as staff and look for either control.
  2. POST /rooms/108/deactivate with a valid reason and a valid CSRF token.
  3. POST /rooms/109/activate with a valid CSRF token.
  4. Re-read both rooms and audit_log.
 Valid session and CSRF for staff; reason=ทดสอบสิทธิ์การใช้งาน Neither ปิดใช้งานห้อง nor เปิดใช้งานอีกครั้ง is rendered for staff. Both posts return 403 with FORBIDDEN_ROLE and the Thai page, and — per O-3 — no digit-grouped amount appears anywhere in either response body. rooms.is_active is unchanged on both rooms and no audit_log row is written. A valid CSRF token does not soften the role check: the guard is requireRole('admin'), not a hidden button. FR-45, FR-02, FR-48 · D-36, D-40, D-47

4.3 Tenants — create, edit, validation and permissions

The tenant record is the LINE recipient and the name printed on every receipt issued during the tenancy. Eight cases. The uniqueness rule is deliberately tested as a database rejection (ENG-1's UNIQUE(active_room_id)), because a service-level SELECT-then-INSERT would pass every one of these tests and still lose a race at the counter.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-ROOM-019 Create a tenant on a vacant room e2eP0 Admin session; room 207 is Vacant — the state TC-ROOM-014 leaves behind
  1. Open /tenants/new
  2. Fill the four fields, select ห้อง 207
  3. Press บันทึก
 ชื่อ-นามสกุล วิภาดา นาคเกษม · เบอร์โทรศัพท์ 0901184402 · ห้อง 207 · วันที่เข้าอยู่ the 1st of the current month 303 → the new tenant's detail; toast เพิ่มผู้เช่าห้อง 207 แล้ว. The row stores phone = '0901184402' (CHAR(10), separators stripped), status='Active', move_out_date IS NULL, line_user_id IS NULL. Detail renders เบอร์โทรศัพท์ 090-118-4402, วันที่เข้าอยู่ in the d MMM yyyy Thai short form with a Gregorian year, วันที่ย้ายออก —, สถานะ กำลังเช่า, LINE ยังไม่ผูก. Room 207 is Occupied. FR-05, D-10, D-07, D-03
TC-ROOM-020 A second Active tenant on one room is refused by the database APIP0 Admin session; room 41 (205) has Active tenant id 37

The post is rejected with 409 TENANT_ROOM_OCCUPIED, ห้องนี้มีผู้เช่าอยู่แล้ว กรุณาสิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่าเดิมก่อน; through the form the field message is ห้องนี้มีผู้เช่าอยู่แล้ว กรุณาเลือกห้องอื่น. Exactly one Active tenant row exists for room 41; the rejection comes from uq_tenants(active_room_id), verified by the error being raised even when two requests are issued concurrently. FR-05, D-10, ADR ENG-1

TC-ROOM-024 LINE User ID is never accepted from a form, in any state APIP1 Admin session; tenant id 37 is linked, room 204's tenant is unlinked

  2. POST /tenants with a line_user_id in the body
  3. POST /tenants/<204's tenant> with a line_user_id in the body

4.4 Tenants — move-in, move-out and re-letting

D-08 makes ending a tenancy a settlement rather than a status flip: the departing tenant must have a final reading and a paid bill before the record can be closed. Seven cases, five of them P0 because each one guards against a room leaving the system with money still on it, or against consumption crossing a tenancy boundary and being billed to the wrong person.

4.5 Tenants — LINE account linking states

Eight cases over the six-character code flow of D-29. A tenant cannot read their own userId anywhere in the LINE client, so the binding can only arrive through the webhook — which makes the failure replies as much a part of the contract as the success one. The alphabet ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789 is asserted character by character, because an O read aloud across a counter as a zero is the exact failure this alphabet exists to prevent.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-ROOM-034 สร้างรหัสผูก LINE issues one code of the right shape APIP1 Admin session; room 204's tenant ธนกฤต วงศ์ทอง is ยังไม่ผูก; today 7 ก.ย. 2026
  1. Press สร้างรหัสผูก LINE on the row
  2. Read the created link_codes row
  3. Open the printable slip
 POST /tenants/<id>/link-code, body _csrf only 303 → /tenants/<id>/link-code/slip; toast สร้างรหัสผูก LINE สำหรับห้อง 204 แล้ว. The row is exactly { tenant_id, code, expires_at, used_at: null }; code is 6 characters, every one of them in ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789 — none of O, 0, I, 1 appears in 100 consecutive generations; expires_at is exactly 7 days ahead (2026-09-14). The detail panel shows the code at 28 px monospace with the expiry as both a date and a remaining-days count, plus the three tenant steps and พิมพ์ใบผูกบัญชี. The row's LINE state becomes รอผูก. 4.6 Meter Reading — period selection and previous-reading resolution

Seven cases on the one query the whole billing chain hangs from: the greatest earlier period for this room and this utility, otherwise the room's baseline. Getting it wrong does not throw — it silently bills the wrong number of units, which is why all seven are P0 or P1 and every one of them states its arithmetic.

previous_reading = (SELECT current_reading FROM meter_readings WHERE room_id = ? AND utility_type = ? AND period < ? ORDER BY period DESC LIMIT 1) ?? rooms.baseline_<utility>
IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-ROOM-042 Previous comes from the immediately preceding period and is read-only for both roles APIP0 Room 41 (205) has 2026-08 rows: water 1,250, electric 5,280; no 2026-09 row
  1. Open /readings?period=2026-09&room=205 as Staff
  2. Read the two Previous fields
  3. Repeat as Admin
  4. Attempt to type into the Previous input
 period 2026-09, room id 41 Previous renders 1,250 and 5,280 as read-only text with thousands separators, each with the source hint รอบ ส.ค. 2026. The value is identical for Staff and Admin. There is no "get previous reading" endpoint to call and no editable control — the field is a dashed read-only input and a posted previous_reading is ignored; the server always recomputes it. FR-10, FR-11, D-14, D-06, D-12
TC-ROOM-043 The first-ever reading resolves to the room's baseline, with previous_period = 'baseline' e2eP0 Room 517 from TC-ROOM-001, baselines 1,250 / 5,280, no meter_readings row; an Active tenant; rates 18.00 / 8.00 / 35.00

  2. Read Previous and its hint
  3. Save the first reading
  4. Run generation and open the Draft bill
 water current 1260, electric current 5350; area 30.00 ตร.ม. Previous shows 1,250 / 5,280 with the hint ค่าเริ่มต้นของห้อง (not a period). Saved rows carry previous_period = "baseline" (a VARCHAR(8) literal, not a null and not a fabricated 2026-08). Units are 1,260 − 1,250 = 10 and 5,350 − 5,280 = 70. The bill reads 10 × 18.00 = 180.00, 70 × 8.00 = 560.00, 30.00 × 35.00 = 1,050.00, total 1,790.00. SELECT COUNT(*) FROM meter_readings WHERE room_id=<517> is exactly 2 — no synthetic baseline row was ever written. FR-13, FR-41, D-06, D-14, D-16

4.7 Meter Reading — entry validation and one row per room, period and utility

Eight cases on POST /api/readings. The below-previous block is P0 because it is the only thing standing between a mistyped dial and a negative charge, and the "no money in a Staff response" case is P0 because D-36 makes it a data rule rather than a CSS rule — a test that only checks the rendered page would pass on a broken build.

  3. Observe the card while typing
  4. Query meter_readings
 water current_reading = 1240 (and 1180 for the UI frame); electric 5350 Both roles get 422 READING_BELOW_PREVIOUS, message เลขมิเตอร์ปัจจุบันน้อยกว่าเดือนก่อน (1,250) กรุณาตรวจสอบ, fields: {"water.current_reading": "ต้องไม่น้อยกว่า 1,250"}. On the phone: the summary banner บันทึกไม่ได้ 1 รายการ, the field takes .field.invalid and aria-invalid="true", the units read-out clears to — หน่วย คำนวณไม่ได้ rather than showing −10, บันทึก is .is-disabled, and the เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้ checkbox stays visible. No row is written for either utility; CHECK (units >= 0) is never reached because the API refuses first. FR-12, D-13, D-16, D-40
TC-ROOM-051 Equal readings are valid: zero units, plus the ไม่มีการใช้งาน flag APIP1 Room 409 has an Active tenant; previous water 1,344
  1. Submit current 1,344
  2. Read the flag and the review screen
  3. Run generation and open the bill
 water previous 1,344, current 1344; rate 18.00 200, not an error: units: 0, flag: "no_usage". The card and the review screen show ไม่มีการใช้งาน with the line 💧 1,344 → 1,344 · 0 หน่วย · มีผู้เช่าอยู่; nothing is blocked. The bill still carries three items, the water one being 0 × 18.00 = 0.00. On a room with no Active tenant the flag does not fire. FR-44, FR-17, D-13, D-18
TC-ROOM-052 Decimals, negatives and non-numeric input are refused APIP1 Staff session; room 41, period 2026-09
  1. Type each value into ครั้งนี้ on the phone
  2. Submit each value to the endpoint

The endpoint re-validates and returns 422 VALIDATION_FAILED for all six — INT UNSIGNED, never a rounded or truncated value, never a float. No row is written and no partial save occurs for the other utility. FR-12, D-16, D-15
TC-ROOM-053 Both utilities are required — a half-saved room is never จดแล้ว APIP1 Staff session; room 108 has no reading for 2026-09; counter reads 42/67

  2. Read the field errors and the counter
  3. Submit both
 water 1260, electric ""; then electric 5350 The empty field shows กรุณากรอกเลขมิเตอร์ and บันทึก stays disabled; a direct post is 422 VALIDATION_FAILED. The counter is unchanged at 42/67 and the room stays under the ยังไม่จด chip. After both are supplied the room becomes จดแล้ว and the counter reads 43/67 — recomputed from the server response, never incremented client-side. FR-11, D-15, D-40
TC-ROOM-054 One reading per room per period per utility — a double submit is a no-op APIP0 Staff session; room 41, period 2026-09, previous 1,250 / 5,280
  1. Submit the save

 the TC-ROOM-049 payload, sent three times Exactly two rows exist for room 41 in 2026-09 (one per utility) after every attempt — the write is an UPSERT on uq_readings_room_period_utility. Units are still 10 and 70: because the previous-reading query uses a strict period <, the row being written is excluded and a re-save can never take itself as its own previous (which would yield 0). The counter never double-counts the room. FR-10, D-12, D-14
TC-ROOM-055 ใช้สูงผิดปกติ fires only above the ten-unit floor and above 3× unitP1 Rooms with the stated previous-period usage for the same utility
  1. Save each current reading
  2. Read flag in the response
  3. Check the review screen's ควรตรวจสอบ count

"high_usage" for 10 → 31. The flag never blocks a save, a generation run or an approval; the review screen lists the flagged rooms with an แก้ไข jump and counts them under ควรตรวจสอบ. FR-44, D-13

The Staff room detail renders ข้อมูลห้อง, ผู้เช่าปัจจุบัน and ประวัติการจดมิเตอร์ only, with no ประวัติบิล panel and no แก้ไขห้อง / ปิดใช้งานห้อง buttons. The same routes as Admin do return the Draft preview 180.00 / 560.00 / 1,050.00 / 1,790.00 บาท. FR-02, D-36, D-40

4.8 Meter Reading — the replacement branch (rollover and swapped meters)

There is no automatic rollover detection anywhere in the product: a dial that has rolled past its maximum and a physically swapped meter are the same input, entered through the same explicit checkbox, with the same recorded arithmetic. Five cases. The first is P0 for a reason worth stating: a meter replacement must not move money.

units = (old_final − previous_reading) + (current_reading − new_start), valid while old_final ≥ previous_reading AND current_reading ≥ new_start
IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-ROOM-057 A replacement reproduces the demo total exactly — money does not move e2eP0 Room 41 (205), period 2026-09, previous water 1,250; water rate 18.00; electric 5,280 → 5,350; area 30.00 ตร.ม.; common 35.00
  1. Tick เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้ on the water block
  2. Enter the three replacement fields and the current reading
  3. Save, then run generation and open the bill
 {"water":{"current_reading":2,"meter_replaced":true,"old_final":1258,"new_start":0,"replace_reason":"มิเตอร์น้ำชำรุด เปลี่ยนใหม่วันที่ 1 ก.ย. 2026"},"electric":{"current_reading":5350}} 200. Units = (1,258 − 1,250) + (2 − 0) = 8 + 2 = 10. The card renders the arithmetic as two lines and a total before saving. The bill reads 10 × 18.00 = 180.00, 70 × 8.00 = 560.00, 30.00 × 35.00 = 1,050.00, total 1,790.00 — identical to the ordinary path. The row stores meter_replaced = 1, old_final = 1258, new_start = 0 and the reason; one audit_log row, action METER_REPLACEMENT, carries the reason and the entering user. FR-33, FR-14, D-13, D-16, D-38
TC-ROOM-058 The two-dial sum is rendered line by line before the save UIP0 Room 210, electricity previous 1,830; the meter was swapped mid-round
  1. Tick the checkbox on the electricity block
  2. Enter old_final, new_start and the current reading
  3. Read the arithmetic panel, then save
  4. Open the review screen
 previous 1,830 · old_final 1926 · new_start 0 · current 96 The panel shows ⚡ มิเตอร์เดิม 96 (1,926 − 1,830) and ⚡ มิเตอร์ใหม่ 96 (96 − 0) with รวมหน่วยที่คิด 192 หน่วย, and the live read-out reads 192 หน่วย รวมมิเตอร์เดิมและมิเตอร์ใหม่. The mode warning โหมดเปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ระบบจะไม่ตรวจว่าเลขปัจจุบันน้อยกว่ารอบก่อน แต่จะคิดหน่วยจากมิเตอร์เดิมบวกมิเตอร์ใหม่ is shown. The stored units is 192; the client never sends a units value. The review screen lists the room under ห้องที่แจ้งเปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ with ⚡ 192 หน่วย · มิเตอร์เดิม 96 + มิเตอร์ใหม่ 96. FR-33, D-13, D-16
TC-ROOM-059 old_final below the previous reading is refused APIP1 Room 41, water previous 1,250, checkbox ticked
  1. Submit an old_final below the previous
  2. Read the code, the message and the field map
 old_final = 1240, new_start = 0, current_reading = 12, reason supplied 422 REPLACEMENT_INVALID, message เลขมิเตอร์ที่กรอกไม่สอดคล้องกับการเปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ กรุณาตรวจสอบ, with the specific field sentence เลขสุดท้ายของมิเตอร์เดิมต้องไม่น้อยกว่าเลขเดือนก่อน (1,250). The form shows the same string under the field with the hint ต้องไม่น้อยกว่า 1,250. No row is written and no negative units can reach CHECK (units >= 0). FR-33, D-13
TC-ROOM-060 current_reading below new_start is refused APIP1 Room 41, water previous 1,250, checkbox ticked
  1. Submit a current reading below the new meter's start
  2. Re-submit with new_start = 0
 old_final = 1258, new_start = 50, current_reading = 12; then new_start = 0, current_reading = 12 First: 422 REPLACEMENT_INVALID with the field sentence เลขปัจจุบันต้องไม่น้อยกว่าเลขเริ่มต้นของมิเตอร์ใหม่ (50). Second: 200, units = (1,258 − 1,250) + (12 − 0) = 8 + 12 = 20. new_start defaults to 0 when the field is untouched, with the hint ปกติเป็น 0. FR-33, D-13
TC-ROOM-061 Reason is mandatory, both roles may record a replacement, and unticking restores the block e2eP1 Room 41, water previous 1,250; a Staff session and an Admin session
  1. Tick the box, fill the numbers, leave the reason empty, save
  2. Supply the reason and save as Staff

 reason "", then มิเตอร์น้ำชำรุด ช่างเปลี่ยนใหม่ 1 ก.ย. 2026; after unticking, current_reading = 1240 Empty reason blocks the save with กรุณาระบุเหตุผลการเปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ (422 on a direct post). With a reason, both Staff and Admin succeed — the branch is not Admin-only, because Staff are the ones standing at the meter. Unticking hides the three fields, discards their values and restores the ordinary guard: the below-previous submit is again 422 READING_BELOW_PREVIOUS, and the saved row carries meter_replaced = 0 with old_final, new_start and replace_reason all NULL. FR-33, D-13, D-36, D-38

4.9 Meter Reading — the round, the edit window, freezing and concurrency

Eight cases on the mechanics of a 67-room walk and on the one rule that governs whether a reading can still be touched. Nothing in this product is ever "closed"; a reading is editable exactly while its room's bill for that period is Draft or does not yet exist, and the word closed should not appear in any screen, message or test the team writes.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-ROOM-062 Round mechanics: chips, floor grouping, counter and no pagination UIP1 Staff session on a 360 × 640 Chrome for Android viewport; 42 of 67 billable rooms complete for 2026-09; 13 vacant
  1. Open /readings
  2. Read the default chip and the counts
  3. Scroll the whole list
  4. Type 205 into ค้นหาห้อง and clear it
  5. Select each chip in turn
 67 billable, 25 unread, 42 done, 13 vacant Default chip is ยังไม่จด. Counts read ทั้งหมด 67 · ยังไม่จด 25 · จดแล้ว 42 · ห้องว่าง 13, and 67 = 25 + 42 — the vacant 13 are outside the counter. Rooms are grouped under sticky ชั้น N headers from rooms.floor and ordered by room code inside a group. The page is one continuous scroll — no pagination control appears at any length. The search filters the visible list only and leaves the chip counts and the X/Y counter unchanged; clearing it restores the chip's full list. The sticky bar reads จดแล้ว 42/67 ห้อง and sits above the three-tab bar without either covering the other. Every meter input is ≥ 44 px tall with a numeric keypad. FR-11, D-15, D-45, D-07
TC-ROOM-063 Editing a reading while the bill is Draft recalculates that bill in place e2eP0 Room 41 has readings for 2026-09 and a Draft bill of 1,790.00; rates 18.00 / 8.00 / 35.00

  4. Open /bills/118 as Admin
 water current 1260 → 1270; electric unchanged at 5350 The card re-expands (it is still editable, because the bill is ฉบับร่าง). The save returns units: 20 and bill_recalculated: true. Bill 118 immediately reads water 20 × 18.00 = 360.00, electric 560.00, common 1,050.00, total 1,970.00 — recalculated in place, with the same bill id and still Draft. Saving succeeds (no READING_FROZEN) and recalculates the Draft bill. Nothing about "returned" is stored on the reading row — the banner is derived from the audit row. FR-34, FR-39, D-22, D-12, D-38
TC-ROOM-066 Revert is refused server-side when a later bill exists for the room APIP1 Room 41 has an Unpaid 2026-09 bill and a Draft 2026-10 bill

The post is refused 422 REVERT_NOT_NEWEST with the same sentence — refused by a query, not merely by a disabled button. The 2026-09 reading stays frozen, which is what keeps the 2026-10 row's snapshotted previous_reading from going stale. FR-34, D-22, D-12
TC-ROOM-067 Two people saving the same room concurrently produce one row, not two APIP0 A Staff session and an Admin session, both on room 108's card for 2026-09, previous water 1,100 / electric 4,200; no bill yet

  2. Staff saves 1,110 / 4,270
  3. Admin, without reloading, saves 1,112 / 4,275
  4. Count rows, read units and the counter
 Staff 1110 / 4270; Admin 1112 / 4275 — issued back-to-back and again fired simultaneously Exactly two rows exist (one per utility) under uq_readings_room_period_utility; the last write wins and stores current_reading 1112, units 12 and recorded_by = 1. No duplicate-key 500 escapes — the write is an UPSERT. Both callers receive a counter recomputed server-side, so the room is counted once whatever the ordering. A subsequent read of the card shows the stored value, not either client's stale copy. FR-10, D-12, D-15, ADR §6.8
TC-ROOM-068 ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ is idempotent and names the rooms it skipped e2eP1 Period 2026-09: 42 of 67 billable rooms complete, 25 unread, 0 approved

 {"period":"2026-09"} First run: {created: 42, updated: 0, skipped: 0, unread: 25, missing_rooms: [...]}, rendered as สร้างบิลใหม่ 42 ใบ · อัปเดต 0 ใบ · ข้าม (อนุมัติแล้ว) 0 ใบ · ยังไม่ได้จด 25 ห้อง. Second run: created: 0, not an error, and no duplicate bill — uq_bills(room_id, period) is the idempotency key. Monthly Billing carries the persistent warn callout ยังไม่ได้จดมิเตอร์ 25 ห้อง: 101, 106, 108 … — ยังไม่ได้สร้างบิล, each code linking to /readings?period=2026-09&room=101, and approval is not blocked. TC-ROOM-069 A failed save on the phone loses at most the room being typed UIP1 Staff mid-round on a phone; 41 rooms saved; network can be dropped

 water 1260, electric 5350 The button takes .btn.is-loading and is disabled while in flight; the inputs stay enabled. On failure: an inline .notice.bad reading บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ กรุณาลองอีกครั้ง with a retry — never a redirect, never a full-page reload, never a lost form. Both typed values are still in their inputs, byte for byte. The counter still reads 41/67 (a failed save can never inflate it). Every other card on the page is untouched. Exit criteria for this section
  • All 32 P0 cases pass on the D-44 seed, twice in a row, with the database reset between runs.
  • TC-ROOM-043, 049 and 057 each reproduce the PRD §28 arithmetic exactly: 10 หน่วย × 18.00 = 180.00, 70 หน่วย × 8.00 = 560.00, 30.00 ตร.ม. × 35.00 = 1,050.00, total 1,790.00.
  • TC-ROOM-068's missing-reading count reaches 0 for the demo period — the single assertion behind "ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่าได้รับบิล".
  • No P1 failure remains open without a written waiver from the PM naming the decision it contradicts.
  • The word "closed" appears in no screen, message, log line or test name produced while running this section (D-11).

5. Billing Calculation & Bill Lifecycle

Sixty-nine executable test cases covering everything that turns two meter numbers into an amount a tenant owes, and everything that happens to that amount afterwards: generation and its idempotency, the three line-item calculations with every edge case the engineering specification names, the Draft → Unpaid → Paid state machine and its illegal transitions, the single approval gate and what it freezes, the two sanctioned correction paths, the derived Overdue state with its date boundaries, and the Dashboard and report figures that must reconcile on screen.

Every amount, status, error code and Thai message below is quoted from the engineering specification and the UI design; nothing is invented. Where a case checks money it states the arithmetic in full, so a grader can verify the expected value on paper without running the system.

Standing fixture — assumed by every case unless the case overrides it
Database
The D-44 seed of §1.3, rebuilt from empty before every case (NFR-23).
Rates
น้ำ 18.00 บาท/หน่วย, ไฟ 8.00 บาท/หน่วย, ส่วนกลาง 35.00 บาท/ตร.ม., all effective from the first day of the earliest history period. settings.due_day = 5.
Demo room
Room 205 = room_id 41, area 30.00 ตร.ม., tenant สมชาย ใจดี (tenant id 37). Its current-period bill is left Draft in the seed so the demo approves it live. API examples use bill 118, payment 96, receipt 96.
Clock
Never pinned. The process runs TZ=Asia/Bangkok and MySQL at time_zone='+07:00', and that is the whole of it — §2.6 rejects every mechanism that could freeze or move the clock, because Overdue is decided by CURDATE() inside MySQL. A case that needs a bill to be due, overdue or aged moves the data with one UPDATE … SET due_date = CURDATE() ± n DAY, and asserts a frozen due date against the expectedDueDate() oracle of §7.8. The PRD §28 calendar (approve 1 ก.ย. 2026, due 5 ก.ย. 2026, pay 3 ก.ย. 2026, REC-202609-000852) appears below as documentation values: per D-44 the arithmetic reproduces on any run date, the calendar does not.
Sessions
admin (นิติบุคคล, role admin) and staff (เจ้าหน้าที่, role staff). "Unauthenticated" means no session cookie. Every POST outside the three machine routes carries a valid _csrf / X-CSRF-Token.
LINE
settings.line_test_mode is ON (D-34), so delivery assertions are made against notifications rows, never against a real inbox.
flowchart LR
  G["Generation run"] --> D["Draft · ฉบับร่าง"]
  D -->|"อนุมัติและส่งบิล, Admin only"| U["Unpaid · รอชำระ"]
  U -->|"past due date, display only"| O["Overdue · เกินกำหนด"]
  U -->|"ยืนยันรับเงินสด, Admin only"| P["Paid · ชำระแล้ว"]
  O -->|"ยืนยันรับเงินสด"| P
  U -->|"ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง, reason 10 chars"| D
  O -->|"ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง"| D
  P -->|"ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน, reason 10 chars"| U
The only legal transitions. bills.status stores three values; Overdue is derived by one helper from Unpaid plus a past due date and is never written (D-21). Every arrow that is missing from this figure is a test case in §5.4 below.

5.1 Bill generation per period and idempotency

One idempotent pass over a period. uq_bills(room_id, period) is the idempotency key of the entire run: a run creates a Draft bill for every billable room that has both readings and no bill, recalculates every existing Draft in place keeping its id, and never issues a statement against a bill whose stored status is Unpaid or Paid.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-BILL-001 First generation run creates one Draft bill per billable room, each with exactly three items API P0 Fresh seed, then reset-period.sql (§2.4.2), which unwinds the current period's bills and their child rows and clears the current-period readings for rooms 108, 312 and 405 — leaving no bills for the period, 64 of the 67 billable rooms read and those three unread. The seed alone does not produce this state: as shipped the period already holds 66 approved bills plus room 205's Draft, with all 67 rooms read. Admin session.
  1. POST /api/bills/generate with the Admin session and CSRF header.
  2. Read the response body.
  3. SELECT status, COUNT(*) FROM bills WHERE period='2026-09' GROUP BY status.
  4. SELECT bill_id, COUNT(*) FROM bill_items GROUP BY bill_id for the new bills.
 {"period": "2026-09"} 200 with {"data":{"period":"2026-09","created":64,"updated":0,"skipped":0,"unread":3,"missing_rooms":["108","312","405"]}}. 64 rows with status='Draft', none with any other status. Every one of the 64 bills has exactly 3 bill_items — water, electric, common. Room 205's bill totals 1790.00. FR-17, FR-42, D-18, D-07, D-23, D-41

 Two identical bodies {"period": "2026-09"} dispatched in the same millisecond. Both calls return 200. The HAVING query returns zero rows. The two responses' created counts sum to 64 with the remainder appearing in updated — a room created between one run's SELECT … FOR UPDATE and its INSERT raises ER_DUP_ENTRY on uq_bills(room_id, period), is re-read, and is counted as updated. 5.2 Water, electricity and common-area calculation

The three formulas and their five documented edge cases. Rounding is half-up, applied once, at the item; the bill total is the SQL SUM of already-rounded rows, so the printed total always equals the sum of the printed lines.

units = current_reading − previous_reading  ·  units (meter replaced) = (old_final − previous_reading) + (current_reading − new_start)
amount_water = ROUND(units_water × rate_water, 2)  ·  amount_electric = ROUND(units_electric × rate_electric, 2)  ·  amount_common = ROUND(area_sqm × rate_common, 2)
bills.total = SUM(bill_items.amount)  — no second rounding, no rounding to whole baht, no satang suppression
IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-BILL-013 Water line — the PRD §28 demo arithmetic unit P0 Rate water = 18.00 resolved at 2026-09-01. Reading row for room 41, period 2026-09.
  1. Save water previous 1,250 / current 1,260.
  2. Run the generation item write.
  3. Read the water row of bill_items.
 previous 1,250 · current 1,260 · rate 18.00 quantity = 10 (1,260 − 1,250 = 10), unit_price = 18.00, amount = ROUND(10 × 18.00, 2) = 180.00. The multiplication happens in SQL inside the INSERT; no JavaScript multiplies money on this path. Rendered 180.00 in tables and on the receipt. FR-12, FR-14, D-16, D-17
TC-BILL-014 Electricity line — the PRD §28 demo arithmetic unit P0 Rate electric = 8.00 resolved at 2026-09-01.
  1. Save electricity previous 5,280 / current 5,350.
  2. Run the generation item write.
  3. Read the electric row of bill_items.
 previous 5,280 · current 5,350 · rate 8.00 quantity = 70 (5,350 − 5,280 = 70), unit_price = 8.00, amount = ROUND(70 × 8.00, 2) = 560.00. The rate is copied onto the item; the row is never joined live to rates by any later screen. FR-13, FR-15, D-16, D-17
TC-BILL-015 Common-area line is priced from the bill's own area snapshot unit P0 Room 205 area 30.00 ตร.ม.; rate common = 35.00.
  1. Generate the bill.
  2. Read the common row of bill_items.
  3. Read bills.room_area_sqm.
 area 30.00 · rate 35.00 quantity = 30.00, unit_price = 35.00, amount = ROUND(30.00 × 35.00, 2) = 1,050.00. previous_reading, current_reading and previous_period are all NULL on this row. quantity is taken from bills.room_area_sqm, not from rooms.area_sqm. FR-16, FR-06, D-09, D-16

The grep finds no FLOAT and no DOUBLE; mysql2 returns DECIMAL as a string with decimalNumbers off. FR-17, D-16, D-03, NFR-13

TC-BILL-018 Half-up rounding is applied once, at the item, and never chained unit P1 Two rooms with non-uniform areas and a satang-carrying rate.
  1. Price 30.25 × 35.55.
  2. Price 30.23 × 35.15.
  3. Price 65 × 8.50.
  4. Assert no intermediate rounding step exists in the SQL.
 quantity/unit_price pairs 30.25 / 35.55, 30.23 / 35.15, 65 / 8.50. 30.25 × 35.55 = 1,075.3875 → 1,075.39 (half-up). 30.23 × 35.15 = 1,062.5845 → 1,062.58 — one ROUND at the item; rounding to three decimals first would wrongly give 1,062.59, and that value failing the assertion is the point of this case. 65 × 8.50 = 552.50 exactly — satang are carried, never suppressed. FR-14, FR-16, D-16, NFR-13
TC-BILL-019 Edge case A — the first reading of a room resolves to its baselines API P0 Room 518, created by this case's precondition — 30.00 ตร.ม., baseline_water = 400, baseline_electric = 1,000, baseline_date = the 15th of the previous month — and given an Active tenant so it is billable. Being new it has no meter_readings row in any period. A seeded room cannot serve here: the seed gives all 80 rooms twelve periods of history (D-44), and reset-period.sql clears only the current period. 517 and 518 are the codes §4 reserves outside the seed's 101–516 block for exactly this.
  1. POST /api/readings for room 518, period 2026-09.
  2. Read previous_reading, previous_period, units and flag.
  3. POST /api/bills/generate and read the three items and the total.
  4. Reopen the Rooms form for room 518.
 water current 415 · electric current 1,120 · rates 18.00 / 8.00 / 35.00 Water: previous 400, previous_period "baseline", 415 − 400 = 15 หน่วย → 15 × 18.00 = 270.00. Electricity: previous 1,000, previous_period "baseline", 1,120 − 1,000 = 120 หน่วย → 120 × 8.00 = 960.00. Common 30.00 × 35.00 = 1,050.00. Total 270.00 + 960.00 + 1,050.00 = 2,280.00. No synthetic meter_readings row was written for the baseline. flag is null on both utilities — ใช้สูงผิดปกติ needs a previous-period usage of ≥ 10 units and a baseline-sourced room has none. Step 4 reopens the Rooms form for room 518: the baseline fields are now read-only; editing them returns 422 BASELINE_LOCKED. FR-13, FR-41, FR-44, D-06, D-14, D-13
TC-BILL-020 Edge case B — a rolled dial is entered as a meter replacement API P0 Room 210, 30.00 ตร.ม. Water reading for 2026-08 is 9,980; the 4-digit dial rolled past 9,999 and the meter was replaced on 2 ก.ย. 2026. Electricity previous 3,100.

 {"period":"2026-09","room_id":(210),"water":{"current_reading":15,"meter_replaced":true,"old_final":9999,"new_start":0,"replace_reason":"มิเตอร์น้ำชำรุด เปลี่ยนใหม่ 2 ก.ย. 2026"},"electric":{"current_reading":3180}} Validation passes: old_final ≥ previous_reading (9,999 ≥ 9,980) and current_reading ≥ new_start (15 ≥ 0). units = (9,999 − 9,980) + (15 − 0) = 19 + 15 = 34 หน่วย → 34 × 18.00 = 612.00. Electricity 3,180 − 3,100 = 80 → 80 × 8.00 = 640.00. Common 1,050.00. Total 612.00 + 640.00 + 1,050.00 = 2,302.00. In step 4 the next period's previous reading is 15, the new dial's number, not 9,999. One METER_REPLACEMENT audit row carries the reason and the entering user. No automatic rollover detection exists anywhere. FR-33, FR-12, FR-39, D-13, D-38

TC-BILL-024 Edge case C — a vacant room produces no bill and no common-area fee API P0 Room 203, 30.00 ตร.ม., status = 'Vacant', no Active tenant — one of the seed's 13 vacant rooms, the same one TC-ROOM-015 and TC-ROOM-046 use. The reading row survives and renders with the badge ไม่ออกบิล. Step 5 returns 422 {"error":{"code":"ROOM_NOT_BILLABLE","message":"ห้องนี้ไม่มีผู้เช่า จึงไม่ออกบิล"}}. TC-BILL-025 A re-let room takes the vacant-period reading as its previous, with nothing billed twice API P1 State of TC-BILL-024: room 203 has a 2026-09 reading of 806 / 2,004 and no bill. A new Active tenant is created for room 203 on 1 ต.ค. 2026, setting rooms.status='Occupied'.

TC-BILL-026 Edge case D — a mid-period move bills the whole period to the tenant Active on the generation date e2e P0 Room 312, 30.00 ตร.ม. Outgoing tenant leaves 12 ก.ย. 2026; a new tenant moves in 16 ก.ย. 2026. Water previous 2,402, electricity previous 7,205.

  2. 12 ก.ย. — run generation for 2026-09.
  3. Approve and send; take cash; check the receipt.
  4. Run สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า.
  5. 16 ก.ย. — create the new Active tenant.

Nothing is prorated and no charge is split: the incoming tenant pays a full common-area fee and the 12–16 ก.ย. vacant window. Step 4 sets the tenant Inactive, stamps move_out_date = 2026-09-12, clears the LINE binding and sets the room Vacant in one transaction audited as TENANCY_END. The ครอบคลุมการใช้ตั้งแต่รอบ … note does not render on the October bill — its previous period is the immediately preceding month. FR-16, FR-17, FR-36, FR-39, D-08, D-10, D-14
TC-BILL-027 End of tenancy is refused until the covering bill is Paid API P1 Room 312's 2026-09 bill exists and is Unpaid; both final readings are saved.

Steps 1 and 2 return 422 TENANCY_END_BLOCKED and change nothing — the gate requires (a) a final reading for both utilities and (b) the bill covering it in state Paid. Step 3 succeeds with 303 and one TENANCY_END audit row. Without this gate the departing tenant's last month would be skipped by the run (the room would be Vacant on the generation date) and silently charged to the next tenant. FR-36, D-08, D-10, D-39

5.3 Rate resolution and rate change

rates is append-only and effective-dated. Resolution is keyed to the period, never to the moment Generate was pressed, so a late second run cannot put two electricity rates in one month and an approved bill can never be disturbed by a later rate row.

  2. Resolve ('electric', '2026-10-01').
  3. Resolve ('electric', '2024-12-01').
 period first days 2026-09-01, 2026-10-01, 2024-12-01. Step 1 returns 8.00 — 2026-10-01 > 2026-09-01, so the newer row is not eligible. Step 2 returns 8.50. Step 3 returns no row, which the caller turns into 422 RATE_NOT_FOUND for the whole run. FR-08, D-17

5.4 The bill state machine and its illegal transitions

bills.status stores exactly Draft, Unpaid, Paid. Every transition is a conditional UPDATE … WHERE id = ? AND status = ?, so the guard survives a double-click, a forgotten handler check and a hand-typed statement. There is no Cancelled, no Void and no fifth status.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-BILL-034 Happy path — the whole PRD §28 lifecycle e2e P0 Fresh seed; no clock is pinned. Room 205's current-period bill is the seed's one Draft at 1,790.00. Admin session.
  1. Open /bills/118 and read the stepper and the Draft banner.
  2. Press อนุมัติและส่งบิล, confirm the dialog.
  3. Open /payments?q=205 — the payment happens the same day; nothing needs the calendar to move.
  4. Press ยืนยันรับเงินสด and confirm.
  5. Read the receipt and the bill's audit timeline.
 Bill 118, room 205, total 1,790.00, due day 5. Status track ฉบับร่าง → รอชำระ → ชำระแล้ว, i.e. Draft → Unpaid → Paid. Due date frozen at expectedDueDate(PERIOD, 5) = MAX(day 5 of the period month, today + 3), computed by the §7.8 oracle and never read as the literal 2026-09-05, which holds only for an approval on or before the 2nd. Confirm dialog body reads อนุมัติและส่งบิลห้อง 205 ผ่าน LINE — ยกเลิกไม่ได้; the payment dialog reads ยืนยันรับเงินสด 1,790 บาท · ห้อง 205 · รอบ กันยายน 2026 และออกใบเสร็จ. One payments row of exactly 1790.00 with method='cash', receipt REC-202609-000852, redirect 303 → /receipts/96. Timeline carries BILL_APPROVE and PAYMENT_CONFIRM as the two key events. NFR-07: confirm to printable receipt on screen in ≤ 5 s. FR-18, FR-22, FR-23, FR-24, FR-39, D-19, D-20, D-24, D-26
TC-BILL-035 Overdue is derived and never stored API P0 Bill 118 Unpaid, then aged with one statement: UPDATE bills SET due_date = CURDATE() - INTERVAL 3 DAY WHERE id = 118. No clock is touched (§2.6).

Step 2: "status": "Unpaid", "display_status": "Overdue", produced by CASE WHEN status='Unpaid' AND due_date < CURDATE() THEN 'Overdue' ELSE status END. Step 3: the badge reads เกินกำหนด and carries data-status="Overdue". Step 4 returns the same bill. No late fee, interest or penalty is added — the total is still 1,790.00. TC-BILL-041 The display-status helper produces all four values and only those unit P1 lib/billStatus.js and the SQL CASE expression, exercised side by side.

Results in order: Draft (ฉบับร่าง), Unpaid (รอชำระ), Overdue (เกินกำหนด), Paid (ชำระแล้ว). SQL and JS agree on all four. A Paid bill never displays as Overdue however far past its due date. 5.5 Approve & Send — preconditions and immutability

One gate. Reviewing the readings and approving the bill are the same act; there is no separate reading-approval step and no submitted state. Everything upstream of approval stays editable; everything downstream is frozen, and the guard is a WHERE clause on every statement rather than a disabled button.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-BILL-042 Approval freezes the bill, sets the due date and queues exactly one message, in one transaction API P0 Bill 118 Draft, three items, total 1,790.00. No clock is pinned. Tenant's LINE binding present, test recipient whitelisted (§2.5). Admin session.
  1. POST /api/bills/approve.
  2. Read the response.
  3. Read bills, audit_log and notifications for bill 118.
  4. Call POST /api/notifications/drain until remaining is 0.
 {"bill_ids": [118]} 200 {"data":{"approved":1,"already_approved":0,"not_found":0,"queued":1,"skipped_no_line":0,"bills":[{"id":118,"room_code":"205","status":"Unpaid","due_date":"<expectedDueDate(PERIOD,5)>","notification":"queued"}]}} — the due date asserted against the §7.8 oracle, never the literal 2026-09-05. In one transaction: status Unpaid, approved_by and approved_at set, due_date frozen to that same computed value, one BILL_APPROVE audit row with before/after JSON and bill_id, one notifications row of type BILL with status='queued'. The push happens only after commit. Drain returns {"sent":1,"failed":0,"skipped_no_line":0,"remaining":0,"lock":"acquired"}. NFR-05: delivered in ≤ 30 s. FR-18, FR-19, FR-32, FR-39, D-19, D-20, D-22, D-30, D-38
TC-BILL-043 The due-date rule, including the late-approval and month-end branches unit P0 The approval UPDATE's GREATEST(LEAST(…), DATE_ADD(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 3 DAY)) expression, evaluated directly.
  1. Evaluate each row of the test data.
  2. Assert the frozen value.
  3. Assert no per-bill due-date field exists on any form or endpoint.
  4. Assert every bill in one batch approved the same day shares one date.
 (period, due_day, approval date): 2026-09, 5, 1 ก.ย. 2026 · 2026-09, 5, 4 ก.ย. 2026 · 2026-09, 5, 20 ก.ย. 2026 · 2026-02, 28, 26 ก.พ. 2026. Frozen due dates in order: 2026-09-05 (MAX of 09-05 and 09-04 — the PRD §28 value); 2026-09-07 (MAX of 09-05 and 09-07); 2026-09-23; 2026-03-01 (MAX of 02-28 and 03-01). The three-day floor guarantees no unpayable window and guarantees the D−2 reminder can never be scheduled in the past. due_day is CHECK-constrained 1–28 so the LEAST(…, LAST_DAY(…)) clamp can never bite; it is written anyway. No endpoint accepts a due date. FR-18, D-20, D-33

  3. Read the Monthly Billing delivery badge and the result page.

 One bill_ids array containing room 211's bill id. 200 with approved: 1, queued: 0, skipped_no_line: 1. The bill is Unpaid, counted in the period's ยอดเรียกเก็บ, and its money is correct. The notification row carries status='skipped_no_line' and is never pushed, so it can never be part of the drain denominator. The badge reads ยังไม่ได้ส่ง — ไม่มี LINE; the result page lists the room with พิมพ์ใบแจ้งหนี้ one click away and the hint ผูก LINE ให้ผู้เช่าได้ที่หน้า ผู้เช่า แล้วกด ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง ที่หน้ารายละเอียดบิล. The A5 ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย carries the letterhead, ห้อง, ชื่อผู้เช่า, รอบบิล, กำหนดชำระ and the three line items — and no receipt number, no payment date, no ผู้รับเงิน and no signature line. FR-19, FR-32, FR-38, D-19, D-28, D-30
TC-BILL-049 Unread rooms never block approval API P1 reset-period.sql applied and generation run: period 2026-09 holds 64 Draft bills, rooms 108, 312, 405 unread.
  1. Select all 64 Draft rows and approve.
  2. Read the result and the warn callout.
  3. Read the completeness line.
 64 bill_ids. 200 with approved: 64. The unread-rooms callout is a warn, not a block: it still reads ยังไม่ได้จดมิเตอร์ 3 ห้อง: 108, 312, 405 — ยังไม่ได้สร้างบิล with the sub-line อนุมัติบิลที่มีอยู่ได้ตามปกติ เมื่อจดมิเตอร์ครบแล้วให้กด สร้าง/อัปเดตบิลฉบับร่าง อีกครั้ง. The completeness line reads บิลครบทุกห้อง 64/67 · อนุมัติแล้ว 64 · ชำระแล้ว 0; billable − billed must reach 0 for the period to be complete, and that single figure drives both the banner and the line so the two can never disagree. FR-18, FR-42, D-11, D-23

5.6 Corrections after approval

One reversible path covers both cases and keeps exactly three stored statuses. An Unpaid or Overdue bill is reverted to Draft; a Paid bill is a counter transaction — the cash goes back across the counter first, then the payment is voided, then the bill is reverted. The system never holds a credit, a refund or a balance.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-BILL-050 Revert, correct, re-approve — the tenant receives BILL_CORRECTED e2e P0 Bill 118 Unpaid at 1,790.00; the water reading was typed as 1,260 when the dial said 1,256. Previous water is the standing fixture's 1,250. No later bill exists for room 205.
  1. On /bills/118 press ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง, type the reason, confirm.
  2. Assert the bill and the readings.
  3. Correct the water reading to 1,256 on /readings.
  4. Re-approve bill 118 and drain.
  5. Read the notification rows and the audit timeline.
 reason จดเลขมิเตอร์น้ำผิด 1,260 เป็น 1,256; corrected water current 1,256 against previous 1,250. Status returns to Draft with approved_by and approved_at cleared; the readings unfreeze; a BILL_REVERT audit row carries the reason and before/after JSON. The flash reads ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่างแล้ว · เหตุผล: จดเลขมิเตอร์น้ำผิด 1,260 เป็น 1,256. The corrected bill: 1,256 − 1,250 = 6 หน่วย → 6 × 18.00 = 108.00, total 108.00 + 560.00 + 1,050.00 = 1,718.00 — the same arithmetic TC-BILL-004 performs, on the standing fixture, with nothing substituted. A correction downwards past the previous reading is not this case: 1,206 against a previous of 1,250 is refused 422 READING_BELOW_PREVIOUS by design, and TC-BILL-021 owns that assertion. Re-approval sends type BILL_CORRECTED, the BILL body prefixed 🔄 แก้ไขบิล (ยกเลิกยอดเดิม). Its dedupe_key differs from the original by type, so a same-day correction is not swallowed by the earlier BILL row. The bill keeps its id, so the timeline is one indexed query on (bill_id, created_at). FR-34, FR-18, FR-19, FR-39, D-22, D-31, D-38
TC-BILL-051 Only a room's most recent bill may be reverted API P1 Room 205 has an Unpaid bill for 2026-08 and any bill for 2026-09.

  4. Assert the refusal is server-side by posting directly, bypassing the disabled button.

The guard is SELECT 1 FROM bills WHERE room_id = ? AND period > ? LIMIT 1 inside the transaction, so step 4 is refused identically. The restriction is not a convenience: a later bill's previous_reading is a snapshot of the value about to be edited, so an older edit would leave the chain stale. The disabled button carries the same sentence as its title. FR-34, D-22, D-12
TC-BILL-052 Revert and void both require a Thai reason of at least ten characters API P1 Bill 118 Unpaid and newest; payment 96 live on another Paid bill.

In the shared confirmation dialog the primary button stays disabled until a reason of at least 10 characters is typed, and the server re-validates — the dialog is the courtesy, the service check is the guarantee. The dialog is never the browser's confirm() and the primary button repeats the verb, never ตกลง. FR-34, FR-35, FR-46, D-22, D-25, D-39
TC-BILL-053 Voiding a payment keeps the receipt, returns the bill to Unpaid and sends nothing e2e P0 Bill 118 Paid, payment 96 live, receipt REC-<YYYYMM>-000852. The receipt is stamped ยกเลิก in red across the header on screen and in print. Zero notification rows are created by the void. TC-BILL-054 Re-payment after a void issues a new receipt number; the old one stays taken API P1 State of TC-BILL-053: bill 118 Unpaid, payment 96 voided, receipt REC-202609-000852 voided.

 bill_id=118&expected_total=1790.00; the counter currently at 852. A new number is allocated, REC-202609-000853; 000852 is never reused. The room-history report joins the most recent payment, voided or not, so the evidence that money was taken and returned never vanishes. Receipt numbers are unique and monotonic but explicitly not gapless. FR-24, FR-25, FR-35, D-25, D-26, D-27, D-42

TC-BILL-056 A stale Payment screen produces a 409, never a wrong receipt API P1 The cashier has /payments?q=205 open showing ยอดที่ต้องชำระ 1,790.00 บาท. Meanwhile Admin reverts bill 118, corrects a reading and re-approves it at 1,810.00.

5.7 Overdue derivation, date boundaries and the daily job

The Unpaid → Overdue transition is instantaneous at 00:00 Asia/Bangkok on the day after the due date: no job, no drift, nothing to fail. The 09:00 job sends reminders and never writes a bill status. At most four automatic messages reach a bill in its entire life.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-BILL-057 The overdue boundary is midnight Asia/Bangkok on the day after the due date unit P0 Four Unpaid bills on four rooms, each aged by one UPDATE relative to CURDATE(). No clock is pinned: CURDATE() is evaluated by MySQL and no fake timer can move it (§2.6).
  1. Evaluate the display helper for the bill due tomorrow.
  2. For the two bills due today.
  3. For the bill due yesterday.
  4. Compare each answer with the SQL CASE on the same row.
  5. Assert no job or timer participates in the transition.
 due_date = CURDATE()+1, CURDATE(), CURDATE(), CURDATE()−1; timezone Asia/Bangkok throughout. The first three return Unpaid / รอชำระ — the due date itself is not overdue, because the predicate is due_date < CURDATE(), strictly less, and a bill due today sits on the boundary from both sides of midnight. The fourth returns Overdue / เกินกำหนด with days_overdue = 1. Every total is unchanged: no late fee, interest or penalty. The JS helper and the SQL CASE agree on all four rows — which is the assertion a pinned JS clock would silently destroy, because it would move todayBangkok() without moving CURDATE(). No scheduled job runs and nothing is written by the transition. FR-23, D-21, D-03, D-05
TC-BILL-058 The boundary follows Bangkok, not UTC API P1 Bill 118 Unpaid, aged to due_date = CURDATE() - INTERVAL 1 DAY. The app runs TZ=Asia/Bangkok and the database session time_zone='+07:00'; a second connection is opened at time_zone='+00:00' for step 3.
  1. GET /bills/118 and read display_status.
  2. Compare the SQL CASE with the JS helper on the same row.
  3. Re-evaluate the same CASE on the UTC connection between 17:00 and 23:59 Bangkok, when the two calendars differ.
  4. Grep the client bundle for date arithmetic.
 due_date = CURDATE() − 1; one +07:00 session and one +00:00 session. display_status is Overdue. The Bangkok CURDATE() and todayBangkok() agree on every row; the UTC session's answer differs during that evening window, and it is the Bangkok one the product must use. Failing that comparison is the point. No UTC value ever reaches a user, dates render as 5 ก.ย. 2026 in lists and 5 กันยายน 2026 in LINE and on the receipt, times as 14:30 น., and no date arithmetic happens in the browser. FR-23, D-03, D-21, NFR-21
TC-BILL-059 The daily job queues PRE_DUE at due − 2 and on no other day API P0 Four Unpaid bills on four rooms, tenants LINE-linked, each aged by one UPDATE. Job token configured. No clock is pinned.
  1. Age the four bills to due_date = CURDATE()+3, +2, +1 and +0.
  2. Run the job once.
  3. Read the notifications rows per bill.
  4. Assert no second row appears on a re-run the same day.
 POST /internal/jobs/daily with a valid X-Job-Token, one call against four differently-aged bills. Only the +2 bill is queued a row, of type PRE_DUE, because the predicate is DATEDIFF(due_date, CURDATE()) = 2. The +3, +1 and +0 bills get nothing — in particular nothing is sent on the due date itself. One run, four ages, is how the production system behaves on any given morning (§2.6.1); ageing one bill through four values and running four times would instead hit the same dedupe_key and prove nothing. The run returns 200 with its counts. A wrong or missing X-Job-Token returns 401 with an empty body, and the login rate limiter never applies to this route. FR-20, FR-37, D-32, D-33, NFR-12
TC-BILL-060 The daily job queues OVERDUE on days 1, 8 and 15 only API P0 Seventeen Unpaid bills on seventeen rooms, never paid, each aged by one UPDATE. No clock is pinned.
  1. Age the seventeen bills to due_date = CURDATE() − n for n = 1 … 17.
  2. Run the job once.
  3. Record every notifications row created, per bill.
  4. Assert every bill's stored status afterwards.
 Seventeen differently-aged bills against one job run; DATEDIFF(CURDATE(), due_date) running 1 … 17. Exactly three OVERDUE rows, one each on the bills aged 1, 8 and 15 days. The other fourteen bills get nothing; nothing at all is automatic after day 15. With its earlier BILL and PRE_DUE rows a bill has received at most four automatic messages in its entire life. Every bills.status is still Unpaid — the job never writes a bill status — and no amount moved. Seventeen bills and one run, not one bill and seventeen runs: notifications.dedupe_key includes send_date, so re-ageing one bill and re-running UPSERTs the same row and would hide the very ladder under test (§2.6.1). FR-21, FR-23, FR-37, D-21, D-33
TC-BILL-061 A second scheduled run on the same date is a no-op that still returns 200 API P1 Today's 09:00 daily run has already completed.
  1. Call POST /internal/jobs/daily again the same day.
  2. Read the body.
  3. Count job_runs rows for the date and notifications rows created.
 Same token, same calendar date. 200 with {"data":{"job":"daily","run_date":"<CURDATE()>","already_ran":true,"pre_due":0,"overdue":0,"retried":0,"sent":0,"failed":0,"skipped_no_line":0}} — not an error, because a retrying scheduler must not see one. Exactly one job_runs row for the date, guarded by uq_job_runs(job_name, run_date). No second message is queued or sent. FR-37, D-32, NFR-15, NFR-22
TC-BILL-062 The manual trigger bypasses the daily guard but not the dedupe key API P1 Bill 118 aged to due_date = CURDATE() + INTERVAL 2 DAY, and today's 09:00 daily run has already queued and sent its one PRE_DUE. Admin on Settings that afternoon. No clock is pinned.

  2. Read the flash.
  3. Count job_runs rows and notifications rows for bill 118 on that date.
 POST /settings/run-daily-job, Admin session. The run executes the identical code body with job_name = 'daily_manual' and skips the job_runs guard entirely, so a 14:00 demo still works after the 09:00 cron. The flash reads ส่งแจ้งเตือนก่อนครบกำหนด {a} ราย · เลยกำหนด {b} ราย · ส่งซ้ำที่ล้มเหลว {c} ราย. Bill 118 still has exactly one PRE_DUE row for today: the generated dedupe_key PRE_DUE:118:0:37:<send_date> is rejected by uq_notifications(dedupe_key), so the tenant is not messaged twice. Two job_runs rows exist for the date, one daily and one daily_manual. FR-37, FR-32, D-32, D-30, ENG-3
TC-BILL-063 Paid bills and voided-payment bills are excluded from the chase API P1 Bill A is Paid. Bill B is Unpaid with a voided payment. Bill C is plainly Unpaid. Each exists in three copies aged to due_date = CURDATE() − 1, − 8 and − 15 on separate rooms, and every tenant is linked. No clock is pinned.
  1. Run the job once against all nine bills.
  2. Read the notifications rows per bill.
  3. Read bill B's badge on Bill Detail.
  4. Press ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง on bill B four times.
  5. Force three consecutive push failures on one Bill C copy and run the job again the next day.
 Three bill kinds × three ages, one job run. Bill A receives nothing — the eligibility predicate is status = 'Unpaid'. Bill B receives nothing: NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM payments p WHERE p.bill_id = b.id AND p.voided_at IS NOT NULL) suppresses it, and it displays the badge ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ — the system never chases a tenant who paid and did nothing wrong. Each of the three Bill C copies receives exactly one OVERDUE row — three in total, one per age, from the single run. Step 4: the Admin's POST /bills/:id/notify is uncapped and works on bill B all four times. Step 5: automatic retries stop at attempt_count = 3 and the job then leaves it alone; the manual button still works. FR-21, FR-32, FR-35, D-25, D-30, D-33

5.8 Dashboard KPI formulas and report figures

"Approved" throughout this section means the stored status is Unpaid or Paid; Draft bills are excluded from every money figure. Reading the stored column for that purpose is explicitly not a violation of the display rule.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-BILL-064 The two Dashboard identities reconcile on screen API P0 Period 2026-09 with a mix of Draft, Unpaid, Overdue and Paid bills. Admin session.
  1. GET /dashboard?period=2026-09.
  2. Compute water_total + electric_total + common_total and compare with billed_total.
  3. Compute billed_total − collected_total and compare with outstanding_total.
  4. Sum the period's Unpaid bills independently and compare.
  5. Assert every bill of the period has exactly three items.
 The full 80-room seed for 2026-09. Identity 1 holds exactly: the three item tiles sum to billed_total, because every generated bill always carries exactly three items even at 0 units and 0.00. Identity 2 holds exactly, and outstanding_total equals the independent sum of the period's Unpaid bills — the ones displaying as รอชำระ and เกินกำหนด — since Unpaid and Paid are the only two approved statuses. A bill with fewer than three items fails step 5 and is a defect. ห้องทั้งหมด 80, มีผู้เช่า 67, ห้องว่าง 13, and the third is computed as the difference so the three always reconcile. FR-28, FR-17, D-41, D-18, D-44
TC-BILL-065 The common-area tile moves from 69,300.00 to 70,350.00 when room 205 is approved API P0 Freshly reset seed: 66 bills of the current period are approved, room 205's is still Draft, 13 rooms vacant.
  1. GET /dashboard?period=2026-09 and read common_total.
  2. Approve bill 118.
  3. Re-read common_total and billed_total.
 67 occupied rooms × 30.00 ตร.ม. × 35.00 บาท. Before: common_total = "69300.00" — 66 × 30 × 35 = 69,300.00, because the Draft bill is excluded and the 13 vacant rooms produce no common item at all. After: common_total = "70350.00" — 67 × 30 × 35 = 70,350.00, the PRD §16 headline reproduced exactly, and billed_total rises by 1,790.00. Both numbers are assertable; which one applies depends only on whether the approval has happened. FR-28, FR-16, D-41, D-07, D-44
TC-BILL-066 Draft exclusion, payment-date independence and the all-time arrears tile API P1 Period 2026-09: 3 Draft bills totalling 5,886.00. A 2026-08 bill paid on 3 ก.ย. 2026. Unpaid bills exist in 2026-07 and 2026-08.

The August payment counts in August's collected_total, not September's — collection is counted by billing period, never by payment date. arrears_all_time equals SUM(total) over every Unpaid bill of every period and is explicitly not period-scoped, so it exceeds September's outstanding_total. FR-28, FR-29, D-41, D-42

TC-BILL-068 Report 1 and report 2 identities, and the CSV export API P1 Twelve periods of history plus the current period, whose bill is aged with UPDATE bills SET due_date = CURDATE() - INTERVAL 15 DAY so it displays เกินกำหนด. No clock is pinned. Admin session.

Report 2: the four non-overlapping buckets 1-7 / 8-30 / 31-60 / 61+ วัน — counts sum to the row count and amounts to the report total, because the rows are Unpaid bills with due_date < CURDATE(); the not-yet-due residual appears above the table as ยังไม่ครบกำหนด {n} ใบ · {amount} บาท and nothing becomes invisible. A bill whose payment was voided still appears with ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ. TC-BILL-069 Report 3 reproduces the demo row, and every chart gap is null rather than 0 API P1 Room 205 (room_id 41) with the PRD §28 bill approved and paid on 3 ก.ย. 2026. One room has a month with no bill at all.
  1. GET /reports/room-history?room_id=41&period=2026-09.
  2. Read the 2026-09 row.
  3. GET /api/dashboard/charts?period=2026-09.
  4. Check every series length and the empty month's value.
  5. Check the doughnut's second slice label.
 Room 41, twelve periods ending 2026-09. The 2026-09 row reads 10 / 70 / 180.00 / 560.00 / 1,790.00 / Paid / 2026-09-03 / REC-202609-000852 — the only report using the display_status expression, because it is the only one whose status column a human reads row by row. It is scoped by room_id, so a previous Inactive tenant's bills, payments and receipts stay visible. Charts: labels and each of water_units, electric_units and revenue_collected carry exactly twelve entries; a month with no data is null, never 0, so Chart.js plots a gap — "0.00" means the month exists and nothing has been collected. The doughnut's second slice is labelled รอชำระ + เกินกำหนด, because the split between them is a display rule. FR-25, FR-26, FR-30, FR-29, D-41, D-42, D-21, D-14

5.9 Section coverage roll-up and recorded assumptions

Grouped by area rather than by case. Where this roll-up and the generated matrix of §8 differ, §8 is authoritative because it is the join of the per-case Traces to cells.

Assumptions recorded by this test set
  • The data is moved, the clock never is. D-44 makes every seeded date relative to the run date and §2.6 rejects every mechanism that could pin a clock — Overdue is decided by CURDATE() inside MySQL, which no JavaScript fake timer can reach. So a case that needs a bill due, overdue or aged shifts its due_date relative to CURDATE() with one statement and says so in its preconditions, a case that needs a ladder uses several differently-aged bills against one job run, and a case that needs a frozen due date asserts the expectedDueDate() oracle of §7.8 rather than the literal 2026-09-05. The calendar values written throughout this section (period 2026-09, 5 ก.ย. 2026, REC-202609-000852) are documentation values: the arithmetic assertions (10 หน่วย, 70 หน่วย, 180.00, 560.00, 1,050.00, 1,790.00) hold on any run date, and nothing asserts the calendar except through an oracle.
  • Identifiers are the engineering specification's. API cases use bill 118, payment 96, receipt 96, room_id 41, tenant 37 — and the UI design now addresses the same ids in its frames, having replaced its own bill 1842, room 205-as-id and receipt 1042. Only batch 418 remains illustrative, because no document fixes a batch id, and it is not asserted anywhere. Room code 205, the arithmetic and REC-202609-000852 are the only identifiers that must reproduce.
  • Room 205's tenant is สมชาย ใจดี, following the engineering specification. The two source documents once disagreed on this one name: the specification's seed excerpt and fixture table label tenant id 37 "สมชาย ใจดี", while the UI design declared room 205 → นางสาวอารยา ศรีสุวรรณ and gave สมชาย ใจดี to room 202. The specification won, because it publishes the db/seed.sql row a test actually meets, and the UI design has adopted it — along with the seeded move_in_date 2025-10-01 and line_linked_at 2026-08-15 — moving อารยา ศรีสุวรรณ to room 202. The id (37), the room (41 / 205), the phone number (0812345678) and the name now agree. No case here asserts a literal tenant name as a pass condition — TC-BILL-017 and TC-BILL-046 assert that tenant_name_snapshot is stable across a later edit, which held under either reading — so this section passed either way, and the S3 documentation defect is closed.
  • Delivery is asserted on rows, never on an inbox. settings.line_test_mode is ON in every environment, so every message assertion reads notifications (type, status, attempt_count, dedupe_key). A blocked Official Account is indistinguishable from a delivered message, so the word "blocked" appears in no case.
  • Receipt numbers are asserted as unique and monotonic only. No case asserts gaplessness: a rolled-back transaction consumes a number, which is a documented limitation. TC-BILL-054 asserts REC-202609-000853 as the next number against a counter left at 852 by the seed, not as a general rule.
  • No case reintroduces a PRD §24 exclusion. No rent, no lease contract, no proration, no payment gateway, no card, no online banking, no slip upload, no QR payment, no IoT meter, no AI. The soft usage flags of FR-44 are two fixed arithmetic thresholds and are described as nothing else.

6. Payments, Receipts, LINE Notifications, Announcements & Reports

Sixty-nine executable test cases covering the money end of the system and every outbound channel: the cash counter and its receipt, the LINE Official Account (bill delivery, reminders, payment confirmation, announcements, account linking, webhook security and the notification log) and the four reports plus the history views. Every case is written against the db/seed.sql dataset of D-44 and the demo fixture ids of engineering spec §3.1.10, so a tester can run them in order without inventing data. Each row names the endpoint, the exact input, the exact expected output — status code, error code, Thai message, badge, stored value — and the requirements it discharges.

Fixture ids used throughout

Admin user
1 · admin · ชื่อที่แสดง นิติบุคคล
Staff user
2 · staff · ชื่อที่แสดง เจ้าหน้าที่
Room
41 · room_code "205" · floor 2 · area_sqm "30.00"
Tenant
37 · Active · 0812345678 · linked
Readings
812 water · 813 electric · period 2026-09
Bill
118 · period 2026-09 · total "1790.00"
Bill items
352 / 353 / 354 = 180.00 / 560.00 / 1050.00
Payment · receipt
96 · REC-202609-000852
Announcement
7 · ปิดน้ำเพื่อซ่อมบำรุง
The one rule this section adds to §1.2

Receipt numbers are unique and monotonic, not gapless (D-05 #10, D-27). No case below asserts that sequence numbers are consecutive. A case that did would fail against the seed's starting offset before the application was even opened, and a rolled-back transaction consumes a number by design. The two assertions this section is allowed to make are that no two receipts share a number and that a later payment never receives a lower one.

The other two rules — assert the arithmetic and never the calendar (D-44), and money renders 1,790.00 on a page but 1,790 บาท in a LINE message (D-03) — apply here exactly as §1.2 states them.

6.1 Cash payment — ยืนยันรับเงินสด (POST /payments)

Full payment only, one live payment per bill, Admin only. The stored amount is copied from bills.total in SQL and is never an input (D-24); every duplicate-prevention guarantee is a database constraint, never a disabled button (D-26).

6.2 Voiding a payment — ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน (POST /payments/:id/void)

The only correction path for cash taken against the wrong room or the wrong month. Nothing is ever deleted, the receipt keeps its number, and the cash is returned across the counter before the button is pressed (D-25). There is no refund, credit note or balance anywhere in the system (D-05 #4).

6.3 Receipt numbering — sequence and uniqueness

One continuous counter that never resets, allocated inside the payment transaction. The two assertions the project commits to are that no two receipts share a number and that a later payment never receives a lower one. Gaplessness is explicitly not claimed and must not be tested (D-05 #10).

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-PAY-019 Receipt number is formatted REC-YYYYMM-NNNNNN from the counter value unitP0 None — pure formatting, no database.
  1. Call the formatter with each sequence value and payment month.
  2. Assert on the exact string.
 (852, '2026-09'), (853, '2026-10'), (1, '2026-09'), (1000000, '2026-09') REC-202609-000852; REC-202610-000853; REC-202609-000001; REC-202609-1000000 (a seven-digit sequence is not truncated — padStart(6,'0') pads, never trims). The YYYYMM comes from the payment month and is a label, not a scope. This is the one money-adjacent operation performed in JavaScript, and it is string work, not arithmetic. FR-24 · D-27, D-16
TC-PAY-020 The counter never resets at a month boundary APIP1 Two Unpaid bills for different rooms; counters.seq for receipt at 852.
  1. Take a payment; note the number.
  2. Advance the clock (or take the next payment in the following month).
  3. Take a second payment; note the number.
 Payment 1 in month M; payment 2 in month M+1 Payment 1 → REC-<M>-000853; payment 2 → REC-<M+1>-000854. The sequence continues across the month change: it does not restart at 000001. Monthly reset is the one numbering scheme that could never produce 852 in a 67-room building, which is why the PRD's own example rules it out. FR-24 · D-27
TC-PAY-021 Concurrent payments never share a number, and one payment never gets two receipts APIP0 Two Unpaid bills (118 and 119) for different rooms; two admin sessions.
  1. POST both payments concurrently.
  2. Read both receipt numbers.
  3. Attempt a second receipts insert for payment 96 directly.
 bill_id=118 and bill_id=119, fired together Two distinct numbers, e.g. …000853 and …000854, allocated by INSERT INTO counters … ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE seq = LAST_INSERT_ID(seq+1) read back with LAST_INSERT_ID() — connection-local, so neither session can read the other's value. SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT receipt_no) = COUNT(*) over receipts. The direct second insert for payment 96 is rejected by uq_receipts(payment_id); reaching that error from a handler is a defect, not a supported path. FR-24 · D-26, D-27

  3. Take the next real payment.
  4. Run the two committed assertions.
 Seed offset; one deliberately rolled-back transaction The sequence contains gaps — from the seed offset and from the rolled-back transaction, which consumed a number without writing a receipt. Both assertions still pass: uniqueness (COUNT(DISTINCT receipt_no) = COUNT(*)) and monotonicity (the sequence part is strictly increasing in payments.id order). 6.5 Payment history — ประวัติการรับชำระ

The Payment page's second tab, GET /payments/history, filtered by payment date where the Dashboard tile counts by billing period — both are correct and the รอบบิล column exists so the difference is visible. Four cases: what the list holds, what survives a void and a tenancy, what each filter narrows, and that a reprint from a row is the ordinary reprint.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-PAY-031 History lists a month's payments and its summary excludes voided rows UIP1 Seed loaded; at least one live payment and one voided payment in the selected month; admin session.   4. Repeat step 1 as staff.
 Live payment 1,790.00; voided payment 1,790.00; empty month Columns exactly วันที่ชำระ · ห้อง · ผู้เช่า · รอบบิล · ยอด · เลขที่ใบเสร็จ · ผู้รับเงิน · สถานะ; chips ทั้งหมด · ปกติ · ยกเลิกแล้ว with live counts. Summary reads รวมรับชำระเดือนนี้ (ไม่รวมรายการที่ยกเลิก) and equals the sum of live rows only — with one live and one voided payment of 1,790.00 the summary is 1,790.00, not 3,580.00. Rows are selected by payment date, so a bill of an earlier period settled this month appears here with its own รอบบิล. Empty month → ไม่พบรายการรับชำระในเดือนนี้ · ดูเดือนก่อนหน้า. TC-PAY-070 Every /payments/history filter narrows the list, and Staff never reach it APIP1 Seed loaded plus the demo run, so the current month holds room 205's live payment and room 41's voided one; at least one bill of the previous period settled in the current month.  period, filter=all|live|voided, q, room_id, page Every chip count equals the rows its filter returns, and ทั้งหมด equals ปกติ + ยกเลิกแล้ว. q by room code matches on prefix, by receipt number matches exactly, and no match renders ไม่พบรายการรับชำระในเดือนนี้ rather than an error. room_id=41 returns only room 41 and still includes its voided row. Selection is by payment date, so the previous period's bill settled this month stays in the current month's list carrying its own รอบบิล, and stepping the picker back does not show it twice. The summary รวมรับชำระเดือนนี้ (ไม่รวมรายการที่ยกเลิก) is recomputed per filter and never counts a voided row. Staff → 403 Thai page, and no digit-grouped amount anywhere in the body. 6.6 LINE bill delivery and the notification log

Money state and messaging state are independent: the approval commits first, sends happen after, and a LINE outage can never roll back a bill (D-30). One notifications row per recipient, never per API call, and every attempt rewrites that row.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-PAY-033 Approval queues one row per bill inside the transaction and sends only after commit APIP0 Bill 118 Draft; tenant 37 linked; LINE_MOCK=1 with a whitelisted test recipient (§2.5); admin session.
  1. POST /api/bills/approve with {"bill_ids":[118]}.
  2. Read the response and the notifications row.
  3. POST /api/notifications/drain in a loop while remaining > 0.
  4. Fire a second drain concurrently with the first.
 {"bill_ids":[118]}; drain takes no body Approve returns {"data":{"approved":1,"already_approved":0,"not_found":0,"queued":1,"skipped_no_line":0,"bills":[{"id":118,"room_code":"205","status":"Unpaid","due_date":"2026-09-05","notification":"queued"}]}}. Exactly one notifications row, type='BILL', bill_id=118, tenant_id=37, status='queued', attempt_count=0, dedupe_key='BILL:118:0:37:<send_date>'. No push happened inside the transaction. Drain returns {"data":{"sent":1,"failed":0,"skipped_no_line":0,"remaining":0,"lock":"acquired"}}, at most 20 pushes per call, header X-Line-Retry-Key = the row's retry_key. The concurrent drain returns "lock":"busy" with zero counts and sends nothing. FR-19, FR-32 · D-19, D-30, D-34

🔔 ค่าใช้จ่ายประจำเดือน กันยายน 2026 / ห้อง 205 / blank / 💧 ค่าน้ำ 10 หน่วย × 18 บาท = 180 บาท / ⚡ ค่าไฟ 70 หน่วย × 8 บาท = 560 บาท / 🏢 ค่าส่วนกลาง 30 ตร.ม. × 35 บาท = 1,050 บาท / blank / 💰 ยอดรวม 1,790 บาท / 📅 กรุณาชำระภายในวันที่ 5 กันยายน 2026 / blank / ชำระเงินสดที่สำนักงานนิติบุคคล เวลา จันทร์-ศุกร์ 09:00-17:00 น.
