TC-PAY-037 Send failures map to the right status, and only a real invalid userId unbinds APIP0 Three queued BILL rows; a fake client whose pushMessage can be made to reject with a chosen status.
  1. Drain with the client returning 500.
  2. Drain with 400 naming an invalid userId, against a real (non-seed) binding.
  3. Repeat step 2 against a Useed0000…37 binding.
  4. Drain with 200.
 HTTP 500, 400 invalid userId, 200; bindings U4af4980… and Useed000000000000000000000000037 (1) status='failed', http_status=500, last_error truncated to 255 chars, attempt_count incremented, retried by tomorrow's job with the same retry_key. (2) status='failed', attempt_count set straight to 3, and the tenant's three line_* columns are cleared; the row shows ส่งไม่สำเร็จ and the tenant reverts to unlinked. (3) identical failure handling but the binding is not cleared — ids beginning Useed are exempt, so a stray 400 can never wipe the seeded state the demo depends on. (4) status='sent'. 401/403 also set attempt_count to 3 and are never retried. A tenant who blocked the OA yields 200 and is recorded as sent: the word "blocked" appears in no badge, report or assertion. FR-32 · D-30, D-44, D-05 #9
TC-PAY-038 Automatic retries stop at three; the Admin's manual resend never does APIP1 One BILL row for bill 118 with status='failed', attempt_count=3; fake client still failing, then succeeding.
  1. Run the daily job and inspect the drain selection.
  2. Press ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง on Bill Detail (POST /bills/118/notify) three times.
  3. Compare retry_key before and after an automatic retry and after a manual one.
  4. Age the row to 8 days old and run the job.
 attempt_count 0 → 3; send_date = CURDATE() - INTERVAL 8 DAY The drain selects status='queued' OR (status='failed' AND attempt_count < 3), so the exhausted row is skipped by every automatic run. All three manual presses are accepted — the button is never capped — and each is a POST-Redirect-GET. An automatic retry reuses retry_key (LINE discards a repeat of a push that actually landed); the manual button regenerates it, or LINE would silently deduplicate the copy the Admin deliberately asked for. The 8-day-old row falls outside the seven-day drain window and is not retried automatically. FR-32 · D-30, D-33
TC-PAY-039 One message type per bill per tenant per day, whatever calls the code APIP0 Bill 118 approved once today, its BILL row sent.
  1. Approve bill 118 again.
  2. Run the daily job.
  3. Enqueue a BILL row for bill 118 and tenant 37 directly.
  4. Count rows and outbox entries.
 dedupe_key = 'BILL:118:0:37:<today>' Approve returns approved: 0, already_approved: 1 and queues nothing — a non-Draft id in a batch is counted, never an error. The direct enqueue is an INSERT … ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE id = LAST_INSERT_ID(id) reporting affectedRows !== 1, so the caller sends nothing. Still exactly one row and one outbox entry. The guarantee is UNIQUE(dedupe_key) on the stored generated column CONCAT_WS(':', type, IFNULL(bill_id,0), IFNULL(announcement_id,0), tenant_id, send_date) — a plain UNIQUE(type, bill_id, announcement_id, tenant_id, send_date) cannot do this, because InnoDB treats NULLs as distinct and one id column is always NULL. FR-19, FR-32 · D-30, D-19
TC-PAY-040 Test mode redirects the target but not the record, and the monthly counter stays honest APIP1 Fresh install or fresh seed; Settings not touched.
  1. Read settings.line_test_mode before changing anything.
  2. Drain one BILL row for tenant 37 with test mode ON.
  3. Inspect the outbox target and the row's recipient_user_id.
  4. Read the Settings counter with 195, 451 and 500 sent rows this month.

Settings shows ส่งข้อความเดือนนี้ 195/500, counting notifications rows with status='sent' since the first of the month only: failed and skipped_no_line rows never inflate it. At 451 the line renders in warn colour; at 500 nothing is throttled or blocked — exhausting the allowance silently stops delivery and is a documented limitation. FR-40 · D-34, D-05 #12

6.7 Scheduled reminders — PRE_DUE, OVERDUE and the daily job

One external scheduler at 09:00 Asia/Bangkok calling one authenticated endpoint. Per bill, at most four automatic messages in its entire life: PRE_DUE at due − 2 and OVERDUE on days 1, 8 and 15. Nothing after day 15, nothing to a Paid bill, nothing outside 09:00 (D-33).

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-PAY-043 PRE_DUE fires on exactly one day and on no other APIP0 Bill 118 Unpaid, tenant 37 linked, no payment; LINE_MOCK=1.
  1. Set due_date = CURDATE() + INTERVAL 3 DAY; run the job.
  2. Set due_date = CURDATE() + INTERVAL 2 DAY; run on a new run date.
  3. Set due_date = CURDATE() + INTERVAL 1 DAY; run again.
  4. Set due_date = CURDATE(); run again.
 DATEDIFF(due_date, CURDATE()) = 3, 2, 1, 0 Only step 2 queues a row: type='PRE_DUE', one per eligible bill. Steps 1, 3 and 4 queue nothing — nothing is sent on the due date itself. Rendered text, exactly:
⏰ แจ้งเตือนก่อนครบกำหนด / ห้อง 205 มียอดค้างชำระ 1,790 บาท / กรุณาชำระภายในวันที่ 5 กันยายน 2026 (อีก 2 วัน) / blank / ชำระเงินสดที่สำนักงานนิติบุคคล
The literal (อีก 2 วัน) is safe precisely because the send day is fixed at due − 2; it is not a placeholder and must not become one.		 FR-20 · D-32, D-33, D-31
TC-PAY-044 OVERDUE fires on days 1, 8 and 15 — and never again APIP0 Bill 118 Unpaid, tenant 37 linked.

  2. Count OVERDUE rows after each run.
  3. Read the text of the day-1 message.
 DATEDIFF(CURDATE(), due_date) IN (1, 8, 15) Rows created on days 1, 8 and 15 only — three in total. Days 2, 7, 9, 14, 16 and 30 queue nothing; nothing is ever sent after day 15. Day-1 text, exactly:
⚠️ เลยกำหนดชำระแล้ว / ห้อง 205 ยอดค้างชำระ 1,790 บาท / ครบกำหนดวันที่ 5 กันยายน 2026 (เลยมาแล้ว 1 วัน) / blank / กรุณาติดต่อสำนักงานนิติบุคคล
{days_overdue} is a real placeholder computed as DATEDIFF(CURDATE(), due_date). No late fee, interest or penalty is added to the total by any of these runs.		 FR-21 · D-33, D-31, D-05 #3
TC-PAY-045 A Paid bill is chased no further, and no bill ever exceeds four automatic messages APIP0 Bill 118 approved on day 0, due date frozen; tenant linked.

Paid walk: BILL, PRE_DUE (the bill was still Unpaid at 09:00 — the tenant receives both the reminder and the confirmation later that day, which is correct behaviour, not a defect), then PAID; the three OVERDUE days queue nothing because the eligibility predicate reads b.status = 'Unpaid'. TC-PAY-046 A retrying scheduler cannot run the day twice APIP0 Valid X-Job-Token; several eligible bills.

  3. Compare SELECT COUNT(*) FROM notifications WHERE send_date = CURDATE() before and after the second call.
 Header X-Job-Token: <shared secret>; no body First: {"data":{"job":"daily","run_date":"…","already_ran":false,"pre_due":12,"overdue":5,"retried":1,"sent":17,"failed":1,"skipped_no_line":3}}. Second: 200 with "already_ran": true and every count 0 — a duplicate key on uq_job_runs(job_name, run_date), answered as success because a retrying scheduler must not see an error. The notification count is unchanged. One job_runs row per calendar day, with finished_at and summary_json written. FR-37 · D-32

TC-PAY-048 The job endpoint is credential-gated and outside the login rate limiter APIP0 Job token configured in the environment.

(4) all fifteen return 401; the 10-per-15-minutes login limiter never applies to this route, nor to /line/webhook or /healthz. The endpoint is exempt from session auth and from CSRF by design. 6.8 LINE webhook, signature verification and account linking

The webhook ACKs 200 before doing any work, so a cold start never causes LINE to auto-disable the endpoint — which would silently kill the only path by which a userId ever reaches the system (D-29, D-43). Linking is a printed one-time code; no LIFF, no manual pasting of user ids.

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-PAY-049 Signature verification, and the 200 that is flushed before any work APIP0 LINE_CHANNEL_SECRET set; app running.
  1. Sign a fixture body with base64(HMAC-SHA256(rawBody, channelSecret)) and POST it.
  2. Flip one byte of the signature and POST the same body.
  3. Reformat the JSON (whitespace only) but keep the old signature and POST.
  4. Instrument the handler to sleep 2 s after the ACK and measure time-to-200.
 Header X-Line-Signature; a follow event body; a tampered value such as bm90LWEtc2lnbmF0dXJl (1) 200 with an empty body — the one documented exception to the envelope rule. (2) and (3) 401, empty body, nothing processed and nothing written, one log line; reformatting the JSON changes the signature, which is why the route is mounted with express.raw before the JSON body parser. (4) the 200 is flushed before the sleep: events are processed after the response, so a Render cold start still ACKs inside LINE's timeout. The route is exempt from session auth, CSRF and the rate limiter. A processing error after the ACK is logged and never retried. FR-31 · D-29, D-37, D-43
TC-PAY-050 A tenant links an account with a printed code e2eP0 Tenant 37 unlinked; admin session; the tenant's userId already present in line_followers from a follow event.
  1. POST /tenants/37/link-code from the Tenants row action.
  2. Open /tenants/37/link-code/slip.
  3. Send the code as a signed message webhook event from that userId.
  4. Inspect the tenant row, the code row, the audit log and the reply.
 Code drawn from ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789, e.g. K7QM4P The code is 6 characters from that alphabet — no O, 0, I or 1 — valid 7 days, single use, UNIQUE(code). The A5 slip carries room code, tenant name, the code at 24 pt monospace, the OA QR image and สแกน QR เพิ่มเพื่อน แล้วส่งรหัส K7QM4P ในแชท. After the event: tenants.line_user_id, line_display_name and line_linked_at set; link_codes.used_at stamped; one audit_log row TENANT_LINE_LINK. Reply, via the Reply API and the event's reply token:
✅ ผูกบัญชีสำเร็จ / คุณจะได้รับบิลและประกาศของห้อง 205 ผ่าน LINE นี้
No notifications row is written and the monthly counter does not move — reply messages are free. The Tenants row now shows the LINE display name, the linked date and a ยกเลิกการผูก action.		 FR-31, FR-39 · D-29, D-31, D-38
TC-PAY-051 A wrong, expired or already-used code binds nothing APIP0 Code K7QM4P already used by TC-PAY-050; a second code aged past expires_at; tenant 38 unlinked.
  1. Send K7QM4P again from a different userId.
  2. Send the expired code.
  3. Send a well-formed but unknown code such as ZZZZ99.
  4. Fire two signed events carrying the same live code simultaneously.
 K7QM4P (used) · an expired code · ZZZZ99 (unknown) · one live code raced twice (1)(2)(3) each reply ❌ ไม่พบรหัสนี้ หรือรหัสหมดอายุแล้ว กรุณาติดต่อสำนักงานนิติบุคคล; no tenant row is modified, no audit row is written, and the submitted code is never echoed back in a log or a message. (4) exactly one winner: UPDATE link_codes SET used_at = NOW() WHERE code = ? AND used_at IS NULL AND expires_at > NOW() affects one row for one request and zero for the other, so the loser gets LINK_FAIL and only one binding is written. FR-31 · D-29
TC-PAY-052 Inbound code text is normalised before anything looks at it unitP1 None — the normaliser only.
  1. Normalise each input.
  2. Assert all forms are equal.
  3. Check the shape test.
 "k7qm 4p", " K7QM-4P ", "K7QM4P", "K7QM4", "สวัสดีครับ" The first three all normalise to K7QM4P (trim().toUpperCase().replace(/[\s-]/g,'')). A text is treated as a code attempt only when the normalised value matches /^[A-Z2-9]{6}$/: K7QM4 (5 chars) and สวัสดีครับ do not match and are routed to the auto-reply instead. Without this shape test the system cannot distinguish a mistyped code from small talk. FR-31 · D-29

(4) is ignored entirely and logged at debug, with no reply. No inbound message ever changes a bill, a payment or a binding. TC-PAY-054 follow and unfollow touch the follower table, never the binding APIP1 Tenant 37 linked.

A tenant who blocks the OA is not reported at all: pushes still return 200 and are recorded as sent. FR-31, FR-32 · D-29, D-30, D-05 #9
TC-PAY-055 Re-issuing a code leaves a tenant with exactly one live code; unlink and relink work APIP1 Tenant 37 with one outstanding, unused code.

 Code 1 then code 2 for tenant 37; a second userId Re-issuing expires every outstanding code for that tenant, so code 1 now replies LINK_FAIL and code 2 replies LINK_OK — a tenant never holds two live codes. ยกเลิกการผูก clears the three line_* columns, audits TENANT_LINE_UNLINK, and the row falls back to ยังไม่ได้ผูก LINE with a สร้างรหัสผูก LINE button. Relinking from a new account simply overwrites line_user_id; the old id is not kept. Uniqueness is on the tenant, so one LINE account may hold two Active tenancies — a message to it is two notifications rows, one per bill, and every template names the room. FR-31, FR-05 · D-29, D-08

6.10 Reports — รายงาน

Exactly four reports, Admin only, each on screen and each exportable to CSV through one helper. No PDF export, no report builder, no ad-hoc column chooser (D-42).

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to
TC-PAY-061 Report 1 — monthly billing and collection reconciles on every row APIP0 Seed loaded with 12 periods of history; admin session.
  1. GET /reports/monthly (default: the last 12 periods).
  2. For every row, check billed − collected = outstanding.
  3. For every row, check water + electric + common = billed.
  4. Confirm Draft bills are excluded from the money columns but counted in n_draft.
 Twelve periods; the current period holding room 205's Draft bill One row per period, newest first, with the columns งวด · 💧 ค่าน้ำ · ⚡ ค่าไฟ · 🏢 ค่าส่วนกลาง · เรียกเก็บ · รับชำระ · ค้างชำระ · จำนวนบิล. Both assertions hold on every row. "Billed" means the stored status is Unpaid or Paid, so the Draft bill contributes 0.00 to all money columns while appearing in the status counts. The outstanding column reads the stored Unpaid status and therefore includes bills not yet due — this is a billing summary, not the arrears list. The splits reconcile because generation always writes exactly three bill_items, so the sub-query cannot fan out or produce a NULL split. FR-29, FR-43 · D-42, D-41, D-18
TC-PAY-062 Report 2 — the arrears list, aged into four buckets that partition it APIP0 Bills seeded at days_overdue of 3, 7, 8, 30, 31, 60, 61 and 90, plus one Unpaid bill not yet due and one bill whose payment was voided.

  2. Sum the four bucket counts and compare with the row count.
  3. Sum the four bucket amounts and compare with the report total.
  4. Look for the not-yet-due bill and the voided-payment bill.
 Buckets 1-7, 8-30, 31-60, 61+ วัน Rows are Unpaid bills with due_date < CURDATE() — exactly the bills displaying as เกินกำหนด — sorted by days overdue descending, then by room code. The boundary bills land in exactly one bucket each: 7 → 1-7, 8 → 8-30, 30 → 8-30, 31 → 31-60, 60 → 31-60, 61 → 61+. Bucket counts sum to the row count and bucket amounts sum to the report total. The not-yet-due bill is not a row but appears in the summary line above the table, ยังไม่ครบกำหนด {n} ใบ · {amount} บาท, so nothing becomes invisible. The voided-payment bill is a row — it is genuinely unpaid — carrying ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ. Scope banner: ทุกงวด ไม่ใช่เฉพาะรอบนี้ …. With nothing overdue: ไม่มีห้องค้างชำระ 🎉. FR-29 · D-42, D-21, D-25
TC-PAY-063 Report 3 — a room's twelve-month history, across tenancies APIP0 Room 41 with bill 118 Paid via TC-PAY-001; room 312 with two tenancies; a room with one voided payment.
  1. GET /reports/room-history?room_id=41.
  2. Read the current-period row cell by cell.
  3. GET the report for room 312.
  4. GET the report for the room with a voided payment.
  5. GET with room_id omitted.
 Room 41 · room 312 · a voided payment · missing room_id Room 41's current-period row: water units 10, water amount 180.00, electric units 70, electric amount 560.00, common 1,050.00, total 1,790.00, display status ชำระแล้ว, payment date 2026-09-03, receipt REC-202609-000852 — arithmetic 10 × 18.00 + 70 × 8.00 + 30 × 35.00 = 1,790.00. Twelve periods, newest first. Room 312 shows both tenancies' bills, because the report is scoped by room_id. The voided payment's row still shows its receipt number with the ยกเลิก stamp while the bill itself displays รอชำระ / เกินกำหนด; the join is on the room's most recent payment, never on active_bill_id, or the evidence would vanish. This is the only report whose status column uses the display_status expression. Missing room_id → 422 กรุณาเลือกห้อง; a never-billed room → ห้องนี้ยังไม่มีบิลในระบบ; no room chosen → เลือกห้องเพื่อดูประวัติ 12 เดือน. FR-25, FR-29 · D-42, D-25, D-08, D-21

  4. Repeat with utility=electric and with an unknown utility.
 period=2026-09 · utility=water|electric · utility=gas At most 10 rows, ordered by units descending; ties broken by room code ascending. Only approved bills (stored status Unpaid or Paid) are counted, so a Draft bill never appears. Each row carries the same room's previous-period units and the delta — for room 205 at 10 units against a previous 8, the delta reads +2. A room with no bill in the preceding period renders —, never a fabricated 0. The electricity tab is the same query with item_type='electric'. An unknown utility is rejected with 422 VALIDATION_FAILED, never silently ignored. FR-29, FR-30 · D-42, D-41

 /reports/monthly, /reports/arrears, /reports/room-history, /reports/top-usage · each ?format=csv Each responds Content-Type: text/csv; charset=utf-8 and Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="report-{name}-{period}.csv" — report-monthly-2026-09.csv, report-arrears-2026-09.csv, report-room-history-2026-09.csv, report-top-usage-2026-09.csv. The body begins with the UTF-8 BOM (EF BB BF) so Excel renders Thai correctly rather than as mojibake. Rows match the screen exactly — the helper receives already-shaped rows and does no querying of its own. There is no PDF export anywhere and ?format= is accepted on these four routes only. No response body contains 1790, 1,790, 18.00, 8.00, 35.00 or any other amount — money is absent from the queries that serve Staff, not hidden by CSS. Staff navigation renders no รายงาน entry. TC-PAY-067 Report filters reject bad ranges and reports never confirm anything APIP1 Admin session.

 Reversed range · 18 periods · 2026-9 · 2026-13 · empty period (1) 422, .field.invalid on the offending select, เดือนเริ่มต้นต้องไม่หลังเดือนสิ้นสุด, and the previous result stays on screen. (2) 422 เลือกช่วงได้สูงสุด 12 งวด, the filter not applied. (3) both 422 PERIOD_INVALID รูปแบบรอบบิลไม่ถูกต้อง against ^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$; an absent period falls back to the current calendar month in Asia/Bangkok, never an error. (4) ยังไม่มีข้อมูลในช่วงเวลานี้ with the filters still usable. (5) No confirmation dialog exists on any report or export — the six dialogs are all writes. 6.11 History views — meter readings and twelve-month usage

PRD §18 functions 26 and 30. A month that was never read is a gap, never a zero — a false zero would understate consumption on the one screen the office opens when a tenant disputes a reading (D-14).

IDTitleTypePriPreconditionsStepsTest dataExpected resultTraces to

  2. Open the room whose first reading came from its baselines.
  3. Open the room with a skipped month.
  4. Repeat step 1 as staff and grep the body.
 Room 41 · a baseline first period · one missed month Each period shows previous, current and units for both utilities: for the current period, water 1,250 → 1,260 = 10 and electricity 5,280 → 5,350 = 70. The first period displays its source as the literal baseline, taken from the room's เลขมิเตอร์น้ำเริ่มต้น / เลขมิเตอร์ไฟเริ่มต้น — no synthetic reading row is ever created.  Twelve labels 2025-10 … 2026-09 labels, water_units, electric_units and revenue_collected each carry exactly 12 entries, so the client zips them onto the axis with no length check. The month with no data is null — Chart.js plots a gap in the line; the month that exists but collected nothing is "0.00". The two distinctions are not interchangeable. Unit series count only approved bills (status IN ('Unpaid','Paid')); revenue_collected counts Paid only, by billing period. status_counts counts bills of the selected period, Draft excluded, with the second slice labelled รอชำระ + เกินกำหนด because that split is a display rule.
