Assumptions recorded by this test set
  1. Fixture ids follow the engineering specification, not the UI mockups. §3.1.10 fixes room 41 / tenant 37 / bill 118 / payment 96 / receipt 96 and states that sibling documents reuse them; the Payment & Receipt mockups draw a different illustrative tenant name and the ids /receipts/1042 and bill 1842. Every case above uses the specification's ids, because they are the ones a test can address. The tenant name is never asserted as a literal — it is read from bills.tenant_name_snapshot.
  2. Dates in test data are documentation values; assertions are on arithmetic and on relative offsets. Where a case needs a specific day it sets due_date relative to CURDATE() rather than to 5 ก.ย. 2026, because seed.sql computes every date from the run date (D-44).
  3. No case asserts receipt-number gaplessness (D-05 #10), no case asserts that a blocked tenant is detectable (D-05 #9), and no case asserts a partial payment, a refund, a credit note or a late fee (D-05 #2, #3, #4) — those behaviours do not exist and a test for them would be a scope violation.
  4. Concurrency cases are run against a real MySQL 8 scratch schema, not against mocks. The guarantees under test — uq_payments(active_bill_id), the conditional UPDATE, the counters allocation, uq_notifications(dedupe_key), GET_LOCK('notif_drain', 0) — are database constructs, and a mock cannot fail them.
  5. LINE is exercised through the fake client with LINE_MOCK=1, and assertions are made on notifications rows rather than on the outbox, except where the outbox is the only way to observe an API choice (multicast versus push, the test-mode target, the retry key). The rows are what the Admin screens, the monthly counter and the reports read, so asserting on them tests the thing that ships.
  6. Approve-result and drain counts in TC-PAY-036 assume the seed's exact 54 / 13 split. If seeding produces a different split the case is re-derived from SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tenants WHERE status='Active' AND line_user_id IS NOT NULL, never edited to match an observed number.
  7. Timing targets are measured with a stopwatch against the full seed and are asserted in TC-PAY-036 only (commit ≤ 5 s, delivery ≤ 3 min) and implicitly in TC-PAY-001 (confirm to printable receipt ≤ 5 s). No load, uptime or concurrency target is claimed.

7. End-to-End Scenarios

Seven scenarios. The first is the graded demo and is the one that may never go red; the other six each close a failure that the demo path alone would leave unproved — a date passing, a mistake being corrected, a role being denied, a tenant with no LINE, a broadcast, and a button pressed twice. Each is written so a person can execute it by hand from the table alone, which is what makes it survive the amendment in §2.2 being declined.

IdScenarioRoles / surfacesFixtureProvesPriority
E2E-01 The complete PRD §28 demo — meter to receipt to Dashboard Staff @ 360 px · Admin @ 1280 px reset-205.sql The whole money chain, both LINE messages, the receipt number, the reconciling tiles P0
E2E-02 Overdue display and the reminder ladder, with the clock advanced by data Admin @ 1280 px · machine endpoint due-dates.sql Overdue is derived and never written; at most four automatic messages per bill; a voided payment silences reminders P0
E2E-03 Correction after approval — revert, edit, re-approve Admin @ 1280 px · Staff @ 360 px next-period-bill.sql The freeze, the reason gate, the newest-bill-only rule, and BILL_CORRECTED P1
E2E-04 Permission denial — Staff attempts to approve Staff @ 360 px none No link, a Thai 403 page on a typed URL, 403 on the API, and no amount anywhere in a Staff response P0
E2E-05 Approving a bill for a tenant with no LINE binding Admin @ 1280 px unlink-206.sql Approval is never blocked by delivery; the gap is visible, not silent; paper is the fallback P1
E2E-06 Announcement to selected floors Admin @ 1280 px none Live recipient count, multicast not per-tenant push, one row per recipient, immutability P1
E2E-07 Idempotent regeneration and double-submit Admin @ 1280 px reset-205.sql Generate twice, approve twice, pay twice — one bill, one message batch, one payment, one receipt P0

7.1 E2E-01 · The complete PRD §28 demo

sequenceDiagram
  autonumber
  participant S as Staff phone 360px
  participant A as Admin desktop 1280px
  participant App as Juristic Platform
  participant DB as MySQL
  participant OB as LINE outbox file
  S->>App: sign in, land on จดมิเตอร์
  S->>App: room 205 water 1260, electric 5350, บันทึก
  App->>DB: upsert two readings, units 10 and 70
  S->>App: ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ
  App->>DB: create Draft bill, exactly three items, total 1790.00
  A->>App: open รายละเอียดบิล, review the six columns
  A->>App: อนุมัติและส่งบิล, confirm the dialog
  App->>DB: Draft to Unpaid, freeze due date, audit, queue BILL
  App->>OB: push the BILL message after commit
  A->>App: รับชำระเงิน, ยืนยันรับเงินสด
  App->>DB: Unpaid to Paid, payment, receipt 000852, queue PAID
  App->>OB: push the PAID message
  A->>App: ภาพรวม and รายงาน reflect the payment
Thirteen steps, two browser contexts, two LINE messages, one receipt number. Money commits before any message leaves, so a LINE outage can never roll back a bill (D-30).
Preconditions
Seed applied. reset-205.sql applied: room 205 has no reading and no bill for the current period, last month closes at 1,250 / 5,280, the receipt counter is at 851. line_test_mode = 1 with the test recipient whitelisted by global-setup.ts and by the fixture itself — without it the seed's NULL line_test_user_id takes the §5.6.5 swallow branch, no API call is made and steps 11 and 15 time out (§2.5). The outbox file is empty. Rates resolve to 18.00 / 8.00 / 35.00 for the period; วันครบกำหนด is 5.
Data
Room 205, floor 2, 30.00 ตร.ม., one Active tenant with a Useed… LINE binding.
Period
DATE_FORMAT(CURDATE(), '%Y-%m') — computed, never typed.
Worked example
the graded run
Every assertion below is written against a derived period, but the scenario has exactly one set of values it is expected to produce on the defence date, and they are the PRD §28 numbers. Run on 3 September 2026 against period 2026-09, the steps resolve to:
WhatExpected on the graded runHow the test gets it
Water1,250 → 1,260 = 10 units × 18 THB = 180.00hard-coded — §28 arithmetic
Electricity5,280 → 5,350 = 70 units × 8 THB = 560.00hard-coded — §28 arithmetic
Common area30 ตร.ม. × 35 THB = 1,050.00hard-coded — §28 arithmetic
Total1,790.00 THBasserted as the string '1790.00'
Due date5 Sep 2026 as PRD §28 states it — which holds only for an approval on or before 2 Sep 2026; see the note belowexpectedDueDate(PERIOD, 5)
Payment date3 Sep 2026, cash at the countertodayBangkok()
ReceiptREC-202609-000852receiptNo(852)

Read this table as the oracle's output, not as its input: receiptNo(852) returns the literal REC-202609-000852 for any run in September 2026 and REC-202610-000852 in October, and that is the D-44 rule working rather than failing. A test asserting the literal string REC-202609-000852 is the defect §2.4 warns about — the literal belongs in this table and in the demo script, never in an assertion.

The one place the PRD's own §28 dates disagree with D-20 · S3 · documentation

PRD §28 pairs a due date of 5 Sep 2026 with a payment on 3 Sep 2026, which is consistent and is what the demo shows. But it does not say when the bill was approved, and D-20's three-day floor makes that load-bearing: an approval on 1 or 2 September yields MAX(5 Sep, 4–5 Sep) = 5 Sep and matches the PRD, while an approval on 3 September yields MAX(5 Sep, 6 Sep) = 6 Sep and does not. reset-205.sql therefore leaves the demo bill unapproved and E2E-01 approves it during the run, so on any day after the 2nd the frozen due date is today + 3 and not the 5th. The test asserts the oracle, never the literal 5 Sep — which is why expectedDueDate() exists. For the graded demo to show 5 Sep exactly, the walkthrough must be performed on or before 2 September 2026, and that is a rehearsal instruction, not a test change.

  1. Staff signs in on a 360 × 640 viewport. Assert the landing route is /readings, not /dashboard (FR-01), and that the bottom tabs read 📝 จดมิเตอร์ · 🚪 ห้องพัก · 📊 ภาพรวม in that order.
  2. The default filter is ยังไม่จด and room 205 is in the list, grouped under ชั้น 2. Its card shows น้ำ · ครั้งก่อน = 1,250 and ไฟ · ครั้งก่อน = 5,280, both read-only. Note the counter value n.
  3. Enter 1,260 and 5,350. Before saving, the live read-out shows 10 หน่วย and 70 หน่วย — units only, never an amount (D-36).
  4. Press บันทึก. Assert the toast บันทึกห้อง 205 แล้ว, the card collapsing to a จดแล้ว summary, and the sticky counter reading n+1 of 67. Assert in the database: two meter_readings rows, units 10 and 70, previous_period = last month, recorded_by = the staff user.
  5. Open the review view and press ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ. Assert the result sentence begins สร้างบิลใหม่ 1 ใบ and that no confirmation dialog appears — generation creates Drafts only and is deliberately not one of D-39's six confirmed actions.
  6. Assert the generated bill in the database. Status Draft; exactly three bill_items; water 10.00 × 18.00 = 180.00, electric 70.00 × 8.00 = 560.00, common 30.00 × 35.00 = 1050.00; total = '1790.00' and equal to the sum of the three amounts; every value a string.
  7. Admin signs in on a 1280 px viewport and opens บิลประจำเดือน for the period. Room 205's row carries data-status="Draft" and the badge ฉบับร่าง, and its LINE column renders —.
  8. Open Bill Detail. Assert the six review columns of PRD §10 — รายการ · เลขครั้งก่อน · เลขครั้งนี้ · จำนวน · อัตรา · จำนวนเงิน — and all three item rows. Assert the Draft due-date label กำหนดชำระ … (ยืนยันเมื่ออนุมัติ) and the Draft banner. Assert [data-amount="total"] reads 1,790.00.
  9. Press อนุมัติและส่งบิล. Assert the dialog body verbatim: อนุมัติและส่งบิลห้อง 205 ผ่าน LINE — ยกเลิกไม่ได้, with buttons ยกเลิก and อนุมัติและส่ง — never ตกลง. Assert the message preview is the rendered D-31 BILL template.
  10. Confirm. Assert the badge flips to data-status="Unpaid" / รอชำระ, the delivery badge settles on ส่งแล้ว, and the frozen due date equals the independently computed MAX(day 5 of the period month, today + 3). Assert one BILL_APPROVE audit row.
  11. The tenant receives the bill. Assert one outbox entry, api: 'push', whose text carries 💧 ค่าน้ำ 10 หน่วย × 18 บาท = 180 บาท, ⚡ ค่าไฟ 70 หน่วย × 8 บาท = 560 บาท, 🏢 ค่าส่วนกลาง 30 ตร.ม. × 35 บาท = 1,050 บาท, 💰 ยอดรวม 1,790 บาท and the due date in full Thai month form. Assert one notifications row: type='BILL', status='sent', attempt_count=1, recipient_user_id = the tenant's Useed… id even though test mode redirected the send.
  12. Open รับชำระเงิน, search 205, select the bill. Assert ยอดที่ต้องชำระ renders 1,790.00 บาท as text and that no editable amount input exists on the page (D-24). The รับเงิน / เงินทอน aid may be present; typing 2,000 must show เงินทอน 210.00 and send nothing.
  13. Press ยืนยันรับเงินสด. Assert the dialog names the amount, the room and the billing period — ยืนยันรับเงินสด 1,790 บาท · ห้อง 205 · รอบ … — because paying the wrong month is exactly the mistake D-25 exists to undo. Confirm.
  14. The receipt. Assert the redirect lands on /receipts/:id; the number matches REC-<current YYYYMM>-000852, computed from the run date; the sheet carries all fourteen D-27 blocks in order; the three line items print; ยอดรวม is 1,790.00 บาท with หนึ่งพันเจ็ดร้อยเก้าสิบบาทถ้วน; ชำระโดย is เงินสด; ผู้รับเงิน is the Admin's ชื่อที่แสดง. Assert no สำเนา mark before the first print.
  15. The tenant receives the confirmation. Assert a second outbox entry containing ✅ ยืนยันการชำระเงิน, 1,790 บาท and the receipt number, and a second notifications row type='PAID', status='sent'.
  16. Dashboard and reports. Assert ยอดค่าส่วนกลาง = 70,350.00 (67 × 30 × 35, up from 69,300.00 before the approval); รับชำระแล้ว = 1,790.00; and the tiles reconcile — ยอดเรียกเก็บทั้งหมด − รับชำระแล้ว = ค้างชำระ. In ประวัติรายห้อง 12 เดือน for room 205, the current row reads 10 / 70 / 180.00 / 560.00 / 1,790.00 / ชำระแล้ว with the receipt number.

Assertions that make E2E-01 more than a click-through

  • The total equals the sum of the three printed lines, asserted from the database and from the receipt page separately.
  • Every money value is compared as a string — '1790.00', never 1790 — because mysql2 returns DECIMAL as a string and a numeric comparison passes while the receipt prints 1789.9999999999998.
  • Quantities carry two decimals: '10.00', '70.00', '30.00'. bill_items.quantity is DECIMAL(10,2); asserting '10' fails on the first run.
  • The receipt number's month segment is derived from the run date. Asserting the literal 202609 is a D-44 violation and will go red in October.
  • The frozen due date is compared against an oracle implemented independently of the product (§2.6).
  • Nothing asserts a calendar date except through that oracle and through Intl with the load-bearing -u-ca-gregory keyword — a Buddhist-Era render must fail this test, not pass it (R-11).

7.2 E2E-02 · Overdue and the reminder ladder

Preconditions
Seed applied, then due-dates.sql, which ages six of the seed's approved bills relative to CURDATE() and voids one payment. No clock is touched anywhere.
Fixture shape
206 → due +2 (expects PRE_DUE) · 207 → due −1 (OVERDUE) · 208 → due −8 (OVERDUE) · 209 → due −15 (OVERDUE) · 210 → due −3 (nothing) · 211 → due −1 but Paid (nothing) · 212 → due −1, paid then voided (nothing, and badged ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ).
  1. Admin opens บิลประจำเดือน. Room 207's row renders data-status="Overdue" with the badge เกินกำหนด; room 210's does too. Assert in the database that both rows still store status='Unpaid' — nothing wrote Overdue (D-21, FR-23).
  2. Filter by เกินกำหนด. Assert the count matches a query using the same derived expression, and that room 211 (Paid) is absent.
  3. Call POST /internal/jobs/daily once with a valid X-Job-Token. Assert pre_due: 1, overdue: 3, and one job_runs row for today.
  4. Assert the notifications rows: exactly one PRE_DUE (room 206) and exactly three OVERDUE (207, 208, 209). Rooms 210, 211 and 212 have none — the −3 day is not in {1, 8, 15}, a Paid bill is excluded, and a bill whose latest payment was voided is excluded (D-33, D-25).
  5. Assert the message bodies from the outbox: ⏰ แจ้งเตือนก่อนครบกำหนด for 206 with (อีก 2 วัน), and ⚠️ เลยกำหนดชำระแล้ว for 207/208/209 with เลยมาแล้ว 1 / 8 / 15 วัน.
  6. Call the endpoint a second time with the same token. Assert already_ran: true, every count zero, HTTP 200 — a retrying scheduler must never see an error (FR-37).
  7. Press รันงานแจ้งเตือนตอนนี้ in Settings. Assert it runs (it skips the job_runs guard by design), reports its three counts, and that the notifications table is unchanged — uq_notifications(dedupe_key) refuses a same-day repeat, and that is the layer the manual button relies on.
  8. Pay room 207's bill. Assert the badge follows to ชำระแล้ว and that no further OVERDUE row is ever created for it.
  9. Confirm the ladder's ceiling: age room 213's bill to −22 and re-run. Assert nothing is sent — at most four automatic messages per bill for its entire life (D-33).

7.3 E2E-03 · Correction after approval

Preconditions
Seed applied. Room 206 carries an approved Unpaid bill for the current period. next-period-bill.sql has added an approved bill for room 206 in the following period.
Story
The office notices the water reading was typed as 1,206 instead of 1,260 after the bill was already sent.
  1. Admin opens room 206's current-period Bill Detail. Assert the locked banner lists what is frozen — readings, rates, room area, tenant name, amounts and due date (D-22) — and that ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง is disabled with the title มีบิลรอบถัดไปแล้ว ไม่สามารถแก้ไขย้อนหลังได้ — กรุณาแก้ในรอบล่าสุด.
  2. POST the revert anyway, directly. Assert 422 with error.code = "REVERT_NOT_NEWEST" — the rule is enforced server-side by a query, not by a disabled button (FR-34).
  3. Delete the later bill and reload. Assert the button is now enabled.
  4. Open the revert dialog and type a nine-character reason. Assert the primary button stays disabled and the message reads กรุณาระบุเหตุผลอย่างน้อย 10 ตัวอักษร. POST the nine-character reason directly and assert 422 REASON_TOO_SHORT.
  5. Submit a valid Thai reason. Assert data-status="Draft" / ฉบับร่าง, and a BILL_REVERT audit row carrying before/after JSON and the reason.
  6. Staff opens จดมิเตอร์ for room 206. Assert the fields are editable again — the freeze is derived from the bill's status and there is no reading state machine (D-12, ADR §3.4).
  7. Correct the water reading and save. Assert the response carries bill_recalculated: true and that the Draft bill's water item and total changed in place, with no second bill created.
  8. Admin re-approves. Assert the new LINE message is type='BILL_CORRECTED' and its text begins 🔄 แก้ไขบิล (ยกเลิกยอดเดิม) followed by the whole BILL body — the tenant is never asked to work out which of two numbers is current.
  9. Assert the Bill Detail timeline shows, newest first, อนุมัติและส่งบิล → แก้ไขเลขมิเตอร์ → ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง → the original approval, and that the timeline card is labelled เห็นเฉพาะนิติบุคคล.
The paid-bill branch is a counter transaction, and E2E-03 asserts only its gate

Correcting a Paid bill is cash across the counter first, then void, then revert, then re-approve, then pay fresh (D-22, D-25). The browser tier asserts only the gate — that ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง on a Paid bill is disabled with ยกเลิกการชำระเงินก่อนจึงจะตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่างได้ — because the rest is a five-step money path whose invariants (receipt keeps its number, new receipt gets a higher one, no credit is ever held) belong in tier 2 where they can be asserted against the database directly.

7.4 E2E-04 · Permission denial — Staff attempts to approve

Preconditions
Seed applied. A Draft bill exists for room 205 in the current period. Signed in as staff.
Why a browser
Two of the four assertions — that the link does not exist, and that the Thai 403 page renders with a working way back — cannot be made from an HTTP status code.
  1. Assert the bottom tabs contain exactly จดมิเตอร์ · ห้องพัก · ภาพรวม and that no navigation anywhere offers บิลประจำเดือน, รับชำระเงิน, ผู้เช่า, ประกาศ, รายงาน, ตั้งค่า or ผู้ใช้งานระบบ — the navigation is generated from the same matrix the middleware enforces (D-36, ADR A-6).
  2. Type /bills into the address bar. Assert the Thai 403 page: ไม่มีสิทธิ์เข้าถึงหน้านี้ · บัญชีของคุณเป็นเจ้าหน้าที่ กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคล, with a button back to จดมิเตอร์ — never a silent redirect, so the matrix stays testable row by row (D-40).
  3. Type /bills/<the draft bill id>. Assert the same 403 page, and that the page source contains no digit-grouped amount: a regex over the whole body for /\d{1,3}(,\d{3})*\.\d{2}/ must not match.
  4. POST /api/bills/approve with the staff session and a valid CSRF token. Assert 403, error.code = "FORBIDDEN_ROLE", the Thai message, and that the bill is still Draft in the database.
  5. Repeat step 4 for every Staff-forbidden route in the §3.4 matrix — /api/notifications/drain, /payments, /receipts/:id, /tenants, /announcements, /reports/*, /settings, /users, /api/dashboard/charts — asserting 403 and the no-amount regex on each. This is the loop that would be tedious by hand and is one for in a test.
  6. Assert the surfaces Staff may reach carry no money either: GET /rooms, GET /rooms/:id, GET /dashboard and POST /api/readings all fail the same no-amount regex, because D-36 removes the columns from the query rather than hiding them in the template.
  7. Assert POST /api/bills/generate succeeds for Staff and that its response carries counts and room codes only. Denial must be exact: over-denying is as much a defect as under-denying (FR-17, D-18).

7.5 E2E-05 · A tenant with no LINE binding

Preconditions
Seed applied, then unlink-206.sql, which clears the three LINE columns on room 206's Active tenant. Room 206 has a Draft bill.
Why it matters
13 of the seed's 67 tenants deliberately have no binding (D-44). The requirement is that the gap is visible, never silent, and never blocks the office.
  1. Open room 206's Bill Detail and press อนุมัติและส่งบิล. Assert the dialog carries the extra line ผู้เช่ารายนี้ยังไม่ได้ผูก LINE — ระบบจะไม่ส่งข้อความ กรุณาพิมพ์ใบแจ้งหนี้ให้ผู้เช่า and that the primary button is still enabled — approval is never blocked by delivery (D-30).
  2. Confirm. Assert the bill is Unpaid, the delivery badge reads ยังไม่ได้ส่ง — ไม่มี LINE, and the notifications row exists with status='skipped_no_line' and a null recipient_user_id. A skipped send is a recorded row, not a missing one.
  3. Assert the outbox has no entry for this bill.
  4. Press พิมพ์ใบแจ้งหนี้. Assert the A5 ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย renders with the letterhead, room, tenant name, billing period, due date and the same three line items — and with no receipt number, no payment date, no ผู้รับเงิน and no signature line (FR-38, D-28).
  5. Bulk-approve the rest of the period from บิลประจำเดือน. Assert the result sentence's four counts sum to the number approved, that ยังไม่ได้ส่ง (ไม่มี LINE) equals the unlinked count, and that the failed and unsent rooms are listed and linked.
  6. Assert the seed invariant afterwards: the count of tenants with a LINE binding is still 54. A Useed… id is never a valid push target and the auto-unbind skips that prefix, so a full bulk approve cannot wipe the bindings the demo depends on (D-30, D-44).
  7. Assert Settings' quota line ส่งข้อความเดือนนี้: {N}/500 counts only status='sent' rows — the skipped and failed rows must not inflate it (D-34).

7.6 E2E-06 · Announcement to selected floors

Preconditions
Seed applied. Signed in as Admin. Outbox empty.
Content
The PRD §15 example: ปิดน้ำเพื่อซ่อมบำรุง / วันที่ 20 กันยายน เวลา 10:00–12:00 น. จะมีการปิดน้ำเพื่อดำเนินการซ่อมบำรุง.
  1. Open ประกาศ. Assert the audience selector offers exactly two options — ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่า and เลือกชั้น — and no per-room selection, no unpaid-only audience and no scheduling (D-35).
  2. Choose เลือกชั้น and tick floors 2 and 3. Assert the live line จะส่งถึง {rooms} ห้อง (ผู้เช่าที่ผูก LINE แล้ว {linked} ราย), and assert both numbers against a query computed by the same criteria — never against a hard-coded count, because the seed's 13 vacant rooms are not evenly distributed.
  3. Assert the character limits: 61 characters in หัวข้อ gives หัวข้อยาวเกิน 60 ตัวอักษร; 401 in เนื้อหา gives เนื้อหายาวเกิน 400 ตัวอักษร; an empty floor selection gives กรุณาเลือกอย่างน้อย 1 ชั้น.
  4. Assert the live preview renders exactly the D-31 ANNOUNCEMENT template, signed — นิติบุคคล {apartment_name} with the name coming from Settings and not from a literal.
  5. Press ส่งประกาศ. Assert the dialog reads ส่งประกาศถึง {N} ห้อง ผ่าน LINE — เรียกคืนไม่ได้ with the primary button ส่งประกาศ. Confirm.
  6. Assert one notifications row per recipient tenant, linked to announcement_id, with the linked tenants sent and the unlinked ones skipped_no_line.
  7. Assert the outbox contains api: 'multicast' in chunks of at most 500 userIds — not one push per tenant, which is the mistake that would burn the monthly allowance many times over (D-34, R-02) — and never broadcast, which would reach ex-tenants who still follow the account.
  8. Assert immutability: the sent announcement offers no edit, no resend and no recall, and a direct POST attempting any of the three returns 409 ANNOUNCEMENT_IMMUTABLE. LINE offers no recall and the UI must not imply otherwise.

7.7 E2E-07 · Idempotent regeneration and double-submit

Preconditions
Seed applied, then reset-205.sql. Room 205 read for the period, so a Draft bill exists. 66 other bills are approved and Unpaid; the history is Paid.
Why a browser
The database guarantees are proved in tier 2. What only a browser proves is that the screens report the no-op honestly instead of showing a success message for work that did not happen.
  1. Press สร้าง/อัปเดตบิลฉบับร่าง. Assert the result สร้างบิลใหม่ 0 ใบ · อัปเดต 1 ใบ · ข้าม (อนุมัติแล้ว) 66 ใบ · ยังไม่ได้จด {d} ห้อง, and assert the same counts from the database: one bill for room 205, the 66 approved bills byte-identical, uq_bills(room_id, period) untouched.
  2. Press it again immediately. Assert identical counts and that no bill's total or updated state changed.
  3. Assert the persistent warn banner ยังไม่ได้จดมิเตอร์ {N} ห้อง: … lists every billable room with no bill, that each code links to that room's reading form, and that approval is not blocked by it (FR-42, D-23).
  4. Select room 205 and approve. Then send POST /api/bills/approve with the same id a second time. Assert approved: 0, already_approved: 1, HTTP 200 — a mixed or repeated batch is a no-op, never an error (§3.3.9).
  5. Assert exactly one notifications row for that bill and one outbox entry. A second approve must not produce a second batch of messages.
  6. Open รับชำระเงิน and double-click ยืนยันรับเงินสด. Assert the button disables with a spinner on the first click — a courtesy — and then assert the guarantee: fire two concurrent POST /payments for the same bill and confirm one returns 303 and the other 409 BILL_ALREADY_PAID with the Thai sentence บิลนี้ถูกชำระไปแล้ว.
  7. Assert exactly one payments row, exactly one receipts row and exactly one PAID notification. The guarantee is uq_payments(active_bill_id) and the conditional UPDATE … WHERE status='Unpaid', not the disabled button (D-26).
  8. Reload the Payment screen for room 205. Assert the bill now appears under ชำระแล้วล่าสุด and that no confirm control is offered for it.
  9. Assert receipt-number monotonicity across the run: no two receipts share a number, and the receipt issued here is higher than every receipt issued earlier. Never assert that the sequence has no gaps — a rolled-back transaction consumes a number and that is documented, accepted behaviour (D-26, D-27).

7.8 Playwright implementation of E2E-01

Seven files. Two are configuration, three are page objects, two are fixtures. Nothing is generated, nothing is abstracted for a second application that will never exist, and every selector below is either a route, one of the four hooks of §2.10, or a Thai label that a decision fixes.

playwright.config.ts

import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: './test/e2e',
  globalSetup: './test/e2e/global-setup.ts',

  // One MySQL, one process, shared state. Parallel workers would need a database each,
  // which buys nothing at seven scenarios.
  workers: 1,
  fullyParallel: false,

  // A flaky money test is a defect, not something to retry until it passes.
  retries: 0,

  timeout: 90_000,
  expect: { timeout: 10_000 },
  reporter: [['list'], ['html', { outputFolder: '.tmp/playwright-report', open: 'never' }]],

  use: {
    baseURL: process.env.E2E_BASE_URL ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:3100',
    locale: 'th-TH',
    timezoneId: 'Asia/Bangkok',          // matches TZ=Asia/Bangkok on the server (D-03)
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
    screenshot: 'only-on-failure',
    video: 'off',
  },

  // Chromium only. D-45 targets current Chrome and Edge; Edge and a real Android
  // handset are checked by hand once per milestone.
  projects: [{ name: 'chromium', use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'] } }],

  webServer: {
    command: 'node --env-file=.env.e2e server.js',
    url: (process.env.E2E_BASE_URL ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:3100') + '/healthz',
    reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,
    timeout: 60_000,
    stdout: 'pipe',
    stderr: 'pipe',
  },
});

test/e2e/global-setup.ts

import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdirSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { createConnection } from 'mysql2/promise';

// Rebuilds juristic_e2e from empty, exactly as a student would: npm run db:reset.
// This is the only place the whole database is recreated; per-scenario fixtures
// layer small, idempotent .sql files on top of it.
export default async function globalSetup() {
  mkdirSync('.tmp', { recursive: true });
  rmSync(process.env.LINE_OUTBOX_FILE ?? '.tmp/line-outbox.jsonl', { force: true });
  execFileSync('npm', ['run', 'db:reset'], { stdio: 'inherit', env: process.env });

  // seed.sql ships line_test_user_id NULL, which is the SWALLOW branch of spec §5.6.5:
  // no API call is made, the fake client is never invoked, and every outbox assertion
  // in this tier would time out. Whitelisting one fictional recipient puts the send back
  // on the path the tests are about (§2.5). TC-AUTH-071 covers the NULL state itself.
  const c = await createConnection({ uri: process.env.DATABASE_URL });
  await c.query(
    "UPDATE settings SET line_test_user_id = 'Utest0000000000000000000000000001' WHERE id = 1");
  await c.end();
}

test/e2e/fixtures/dates.ts — the oracles

// Asia/Bangkok is UTC+7 with no DST, so a fixed offset is exact and needs no library.
// These are deliberately an INDEPENDENT implementation of the product's date rules:
// if the two disagree, one of them is wrong, and that is the point of an oracle.

export const todayBangkok = (): string =>
  new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 3_600_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

export const currentPeriod = (): string => todayBangkok().slice(0, 7);

export const addDays = (iso: string, n: number): string =>
  new Date(Date.parse(iso + 'T00:00:00Z') + n * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

/**
 * D-20: due_date = MAX(due day of the period month, approval date + 3 days).
 * The clamp applies to the DAY-OF-MONTH term only — never to the result of the MAX,
 * which would otherwise be able to produce a due date before the three-day floor.
 * See the S3 documentation defect raised in the strategy section.
 */
export function expectedDueDate(period: string, dueDay = 5, approvedOn = todayBangkok()): string {
  const [y, m] = period.split('-').map(Number);
  const lastDayOfMonth = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 0)).getUTCDate();
  const onDueDay = `${period}-${String(Math.min(dueDay, lastDayOfMonth)).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const floor = addDays(approvedOn, 3);
  return onDueDay > floor ? onDueDay : floor;   // ISO dates compare correctly as strings
}

// LINE messages and the receipt use the full Thai month with a GREGORIAN year.
// The -u-ca-gregory keyword is load-bearing: plain th-TH renders Buddhist Era (D-03).
export const thaiFullDate = (iso: string): string =>
  new Intl.DateTimeFormat('th-TH-u-ca-gregory-nu-latn', {
    timeZone: 'Asia/Bangkok', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(iso + 'T12:00:00+07:00'));

// REC-<payment month YYYYMM>-<6 digits>. The month comes from the RUN DATE, never
// from a literal: D-44 forbids any test asserting the documentation's 202609.
export const receiptNo = (seq: number, on = todayBangkok()): string =>
  `REC-${on.slice(0, 4)}${on.slice(5, 7)}-${String(seq).padStart(6, '0')}`;

test/e2e/fixtures/test.ts — the db and outbox fixtures

import { test as base, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import { createConnection, type Connection } from 'mysql2/promise';
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';

export type OutboxEntry = { api: 'push' | 'multicast' | 'reply'; to?: string; msg: any; key?: string };

class Db {
  constructor(private readonly c: Connection) {}

  static async open() {
    // dateStrings mirrors db/pool.js so a DATE never passes through a JS Date (ADR A-5).
    const c = await createConnection({
      uri: process.env.DATABASE_URL,
      multipleStatements: true,
      dateStrings: true,
      timezone: '+07:00',
      decimalNumbers: false,          // DECIMAL arrives as a string, as in production
    });
    return new Db(c);
  }

  async rows<T = any>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<T[]> {
    const [r] = await this.c.query(sql, params);
    return r as T[];
  }

  async one<T = any>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<T> {
    const r = await this.rows<T>(sql, params);
    expect(r, `expected exactly one row for: ${sql}`).toHaveLength(1);
    return r[0];
  }

  /** Applies one idempotent .sql fixture from test/fixtures/. */
  async applyFixture(name: string) {
    await this.c.query(readFileSync(`test/fixtures/${name}`, 'utf8'));
  }

  roomId = (code: string) =>
    this.one<{ id: number }>('SELECT id FROM rooms WHERE room_code = ?', [code]).then((r) => r.id);

  billId = (roomCode: string, period: string) =>
    this.one<{ id: number }>(
      'SELECT b.id FROM bills b JOIN rooms r ON r.id = b.room_id WHERE r.room_code = ? AND b.period = ?',
      [roomCode, period],
    ).then((r) => r.id);

  items = (billId: number) =>
    this.rows('SELECT item_type, quantity, unit_price, amount FROM bill_items WHERE bill_id = ? ORDER BY item_type', [billId]);

  notifications = (billId: number) =>
    this.rows('SELECT type, status, attempt_count, recipient_user_id FROM notifications WHERE bill_id = ? ORDER BY id', [billId]);

  close = () => this.c.end();
}

class Outbox {
  private readonly path = process.env.LINE_OUTBOX_FILE ?? '.tmp/line-outbox.jsonl';

  truncate() { writeFileSync(this.path, ''); }

  all(): OutboxEntry[] {
    if (!existsSync(this.path)) return [];
    return readFileSync(this.path, 'utf8').split('\n').filter(Boolean).map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
  }

  /** The rendered Thai text of every message sent so far. */
  texts(): string[] { return this.all().map((e) => e.msg?.text ?? ''); }

  /** Polls the file — the send happens after the response, so it can land a beat late. */
  async waitForText(needle: string, timeoutMs = 15_000): Promise<string> {
    const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
    while (Date.now() < deadline) {
      const hit = this.texts().find((t) => t.includes(needle));
      if (hit) return hit;
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 250));
    }
    throw new Error(`no LINE message containing "${needle}" after ${timeoutMs}ms. Sent: ${JSON.stringify(this.texts())}`);
  }
}

export const test = base.extend<{ db: Db; outbox: Outbox }>({
  db: async ({}, use) => { const db = await Db.open(); await use(db); await db.close(); },
  outbox: async ({}, use) => { await use(new Outbox()); },
});

export { expect };

/** D-44: the staff password is not specified, so fall back to the admin's. */
export const credentials = (role: 'admin' | 'staff') => ({
  username: role,
  password: role === 'admin'
    ? process.env.SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD!
    : process.env.E2E_STAFF_PASSWORD ?? process.env.SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD!,
});

test/e2e/pages/*.ts — page objects

// test/e2e/pages/LoginPage.ts
import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { credentials } from '../fixtures/test';

export class LoginPage {
  constructor(private readonly page: Page) {}

  async signIn(role: 'admin' | 'staff') {
    const { username, password } = credentials(role);
    await this.page.goto('/login');
    await expect(this.page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Juristic Platform' })).toBeVisible();
    await this.page.getByLabel('ชื่อผู้ใช้').fill(username);
    await this.page.getByLabel('รหัสผ่าน').fill(password);
    await this.page.getByRole('button', { name: 'เข้าสู่ระบบ' }).click();
    // FR-01: admin lands on ภาพรวม, staff on จดมิเตอร์.
    await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(role === 'admin' ? /\/dashboard/ : /\/readings/);
  }
}

// test/e2e/pages/ReadingsPage.ts
import { expect, type Page, type Locator } from '@playwright/test';

export class ReadingsPage {
  constructor(private readonly page: Page) {}

  goto = (period: string) => this.page.goto(`/readings?period=${period}`);
  gotoReview = (period: string) => this.page.goto(`/readings?period=${period}&view=review`);

  card = (roomCode: string): Locator => this.page.locator(`[data-room="${roomCode}"]`);

  /** Each utility block is a fieldset with a legend, so the two ครั้งนี้ inputs are distinct. */
  private field = (roomCode: string, utility: 'น้ำ' | 'ไฟ') =>
    this.card(roomCode).getByRole('group', { name: utility }).getByLabel('ครั้งนี้');

  /**
   * D-06: ครั้งก่อน is READ-ONLY TEXT, not an input, so toHaveValue can never reach it.
   * The element carries data-previous with the raw integer (§2.10) and renders the
   * grouped 1,250 as its text; the caller picks which of the two it means.
   */
  previous = (roomCode: string, utility: 'น้ำ' | 'ไฟ') =>
    this.card(roomCode).getByRole('group', { name: utility }).locator('[data-previous]');

  async enter(roomCode: string, water: number, electric: number) {
    await this.field(roomCode, 'น้ำ').fill(String(water));
    await this.field(roomCode, 'ไฟ').fill(String(electric));
  }

  save = (roomCode: string) => this.card(roomCode).getByRole('button', { name: 'บันทึก' }).click();

  counter = (): Locator => this.page.getByText(/จดแล้ว\s+\d+\/\d+\s+ห้อง/);

  async counterDone(): Promise<number> {
    const text = (await this.counter().innerText()).replace(/[^\d/]/g, '');
    return Number(text.split('/')[0]);
  }

  async submitForReview() {
    await this.page.getByRole('button', { name: 'ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ' }).click();
    // D-18/D-39: generation creates Drafts only and carries NO confirmation dialog.
    await expect(this.page.getByRole('dialog')).toHaveCount(0);
  }
}

// test/e2e/pages/BillPages.ts
import { expect, type Page, type Locator } from '@playwright/test';

export class BillsPage {
  constructor(private readonly page: Page) {}

  goto = (period: string) => this.page.goto(`/bills?period=${period}`);
  row = (roomCode: string): Locator => this.page.locator(`tr[data-room="${roomCode}"]`);
  status = (roomCode: string): Locator => this.row(roomCode).locator('[data-status]');

  async open(roomCode: string) {
    await this.row(roomCode).getByRole('link').first().click();
    await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(/\/bills\/\d+/);
  }
}

export class BillDetailPage {
  constructor(private readonly page: Page) {}

  amount = (kind: 'water' | 'electric' | 'common' | 'total'): Locator =>
    this.page.locator(`[data-amount="${kind}"]`);

  status = (): Locator => this.page.locator('[data-status]').first();

  itemRow = (label: 'ค่าน้ำ' | 'ค่าไฟ' | 'ค่าส่วนกลาง'): Locator =>
    this.page.getByRole('row').filter({ hasText: label });

  async approve(roomCode: string) {
    await this.page.getByRole('button', { name: 'อนุมัติและส่งบิล' }).click();
    const dialog = this.page.getByRole('dialog');
    // D-39 dialog 1, verbatim — and the primary repeats the verb, never ตกลง.
    await expect(dialog).toContainText(`อนุมัติและส่งบิลห้อง ${roomCode} ผ่าน LINE — ยกเลิกไม่ได้`);
    await expect(dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'ตกลง' })).toHaveCount(0);
    await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'อนุมัติและส่ง' }).click();
  }

  /** The result screen drains at most 20 messages per call and loops while remaining > 0. */
  waitForDelivered = () =>
    expect(this.page.getByText('ส่งแล้ว')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 });
}

// test/e2e/pages/PaymentPages.ts
import { expect, type Page, type Locator } from '@playwright/test';

export class PaymentPage {
  constructor(private readonly page: Page) {}

  goto = () => this.page.goto('/payments');

  async search(query: string) {
    await this.page.getByPlaceholder('เลขห้อง หรือ ชื่อผู้เช่า').fill(query);
    await expect(this.page.getByText(/พบ \d+ ห้อง/)).toBeVisible();
  }

  selectBill = (billId: number) => this.page.locator(`[data-bill="${billId}"]`).click();

  amountDue = (): Locator => this.page.locator('[data-amount="total"]');

  /** D-24: the amount is read-only text. This asserts there is no way to type one. */
  async assertAmountIsNotAnInput() {
    await expect(this.page.locator('input[name="amount"]')).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(this.amountDue()).not.toHaveAttribute('contenteditable', /.*/);
  }

  async confirmCash(roomCode: string, thaiTotal: string, periodTh: string) {
    await this.page.getByRole('button', { name: 'ยืนยันรับเงินสด' }).click();
    const dialog = this.page.getByRole('dialog');
    // D-39 dialog 3 — the billing period is part of the sentence on purpose.
    await expect(dialog).toContainText(`ยืนยันรับเงินสด ${thaiTotal} บาท`);
    await expect(dialog).toContainText(`ห้อง ${roomCode}`);
    await expect(dialog).toContainText(`รอบ ${periodTh}`);
    await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'ยืนยันรับเงินสด' }).click();
    await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(/\/receipts\/\d+/);
  }
}

export class ReceiptPage {
  constructor(private readonly page: Page) {}

  number = (): Locator => this.page.getByText(/REC-\d{6}-\d{6}/).first();
  amount = (kind: 'water' | 'electric' | 'common' | 'total'): Locator =>
    this.page.locator(`[data-amount="${kind}"]`);
}

test/e2e/e2e-01-demo-path.spec.ts

import { test, expect } from './fixtures/test';
import { LoginPage } from './pages/LoginPage';
import { ReadingsPage } from './pages/ReadingsPage';
import { BillsPage, BillDetailPage } from './pages/BillPages';
import { PaymentPage, ReceiptPage } from './pages/PaymentPages';
import { currentPeriod, expectedDueDate, thaiFullDate, receiptNo, todayBangkok } from './fixtures/dates';

const PERIOD = currentPeriod();
const ROOM = '205';

// PRD §28, and the only numbers this scenario is allowed to hard-code.
const WATER_PREV = 1250, WATER_CURR = 1260, WATER_UNITS = 10;
const ELEC_PREV = 5280,  ELEC_CURR = 5350,  ELEC_UNITS = 70;

test.describe('E2E-01 · the complete PRD §28 demo path', () => {
  test.beforeEach(async ({ db, outbox }) => {
    await db.applyFixture('reset-205.sql');
    outbox.truncate();
  });

  test(
    'E2E-01 · FR-01 FR-10 FR-11 FR-12 FR-13 FR-14 FR-15 FR-16 FR-17 FR-18 FR-19 FR-22 FR-23 FR-24 FR-28 · ' +
    'room 205 from meter reading to receipt to Dashboard',
    async ({ browser, db, outbox }) => {

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- staff, 360 x 640
    const staffCtx = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: 360, height: 640 } });
    const staff = await staffCtx.newPage();
    const readings = new ReadingsPage(staff);

    await test.step('1-2 · staff signs in and lands on the reading round', async () => {
      await new LoginPage(staff).signIn('staff');
      await readings.goto(PERIOD);
      await expect(readings.card(ROOM)).toBeVisible();
      // ครั้งก่อน is read-only text (D-06), so assert the raw hook and the grouped render,
      // never toHaveValue — there is no form control here to hold a value.
      await expect(readings.previous(ROOM, 'น้ำ')).toHaveAttribute('data-previous', String(WATER_PREV));
      await expect(readings.previous(ROOM, 'น้ำ')).toHaveText('1,250');
      await expect(readings.previous(ROOM, 'ไฟ')).toHaveAttribute('data-previous', String(ELEC_PREV));
      await expect(readings.previous(ROOM, 'ไฟ')).toHaveText('5,280');
    });

    const before = await readings.counterDone();

    await test.step('3-4 · enter the readings and save one room', async () => {
      await readings.enter(ROOM, WATER_CURR, ELEC_CURR);
      await expect(readings.card(ROOM)).toContainText(`${WATER_UNITS} หน่วย`);
      await expect(readings.card(ROOM)).toContainText(`${ELEC_UNITS} หน่วย`);
      await readings.save(ROOM);
      await expect(staff.getByText(`บันทึกห้อง ${ROOM} แล้ว`)).toBeVisible();
      await expect(readings.card(ROOM)).toContainText('จดแล้ว');
      expect(await readings.counterDone()).toBe(before + 1);
    });

    await test.step('4b · the readings are one row each, with the units snapshotted', async () => {
      const roomId = await db.roomId(ROOM);
      const rows = await db.rows(
        'SELECT utility_type, previous_reading, current_reading, units, previous_period ' +
        'FROM meter_readings WHERE room_id = ? AND period = ? ORDER BY utility_type',
        [roomId, PERIOD],
      );
      expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);                       // FR-10, FR-11 — an UPSERT, never two rows
      expect(rows.find((r) => r.utility_type === 'water')).toMatchObject(
        { previous_reading: WATER_PREV, current_reading: WATER_CURR, units: WATER_UNITS });
      expect(rows.find((r) => r.utility_type === 'electric')).toMatchObject(
        { previous_reading: ELEC_PREV, current_reading: ELEC_CURR, units: ELEC_UNITS });
    });

    await test.step('5 · ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ generates the Draft bill', async () => {
      await readings.gotoReview(PERIOD);
      await readings.submitForReview();
      await expect(staff.getByText(/สร้างบิลใหม่ 1 ใบ/)).toBeVisible();
    });

    const billId = await db.billId(ROOM, PERIOD);

    await test.step('6 · the never-red arithmetic, asserted as strings', async () => {
      const items = await db.items(billId);
      expect(items).toHaveLength(3);                      // D-18, D-41 — always exactly three
      expect(items).toEqual([
        { item_type: 'common',   quantity: '30.00', unit_price: '35.00', amount: '1050.00' },
        { item_type: 'electric', quantity: '70.00', unit_price: '8.00',  amount: '560.00'  },
        { item_type: 'water',    quantity: '10.00', unit_price: '18.00', amount: '180.00'  },
      ]);
      const bill = await db.one('SELECT status, total, room_area_sqm FROM bills WHERE id = ?', [billId]);
      expect(bill.status).toBe('Draft');
      expect(bill.total).toBe('1790.00');                 // the sum of the printed lines
      expect(bill.room_area_sqm).toBe('30.00');           // snapshotted at generation (D-09)
    });

    await staffCtx.close();

    // -------------------------------------------------------------- admin, 1280 x 900
    const adminCtx = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 900 } });
    const admin = await adminCtx.newPage();
    const bills = new BillsPage(admin);
    const detail = new BillDetailPage(admin);

    await test.step('7-8 · the office reviews the bill', async () => {
      await new LoginPage(admin).signIn('admin');
      await bills.goto(PERIOD);
      await expect(bills.status(ROOM)).toHaveAttribute('data-status', 'Draft');
      await expect(bills.status(ROOM)).toHaveText('ฉบับร่าง');
      await bills.open(ROOM);

      await expect(detail.itemRow('ค่าน้ำ')).toContainText('1,250');
      await expect(detail.itemRow('ค่าน้ำ')).toContainText('1,260');
      await expect(detail.itemRow('ค่าน้ำ')).toContainText('18.00');
      await expect(detail.amount('water')).toHaveText('180.00');
      await expect(detail.amount('electric')).toHaveText('560.00');
      await expect(detail.amount('common')).toHaveText('1,050.00');
      await expect(detail.amount('total')).toHaveText('1,790.00');
      await expect(admin.getByText(/กำหนดชำระ .* \(ยืนยันเมื่ออนุมัติ\)/)).toBeVisible();
    });

    await test.step('9-10 · อนุมัติและส่งบิล freezes the bill and queues the message', async () => {
      await detail.approve(ROOM);
      await expect(detail.status()).toHaveAttribute('data-status', 'Unpaid');
      await expect(detail.status()).toHaveText('รอชำระ');
      await detail.waitForDelivered();

      const bill = await db.one('SELECT status, due_date, approved_by FROM bills WHERE id = ?', [billId]);
      expect(bill.status).toBe('Unpaid');
      expect(bill.due_date).toBe(expectedDueDate(PERIOD, 5));     // D-20, independently computed
      expect(bill.approved_by).not.toBeNull();

      const audit = await db.rows(
        "SELECT action FROM audit_log WHERE bill_id = ? AND action = 'BILL_APPROVE'", [billId]);
      expect(audit).toHaveLength(1);                               // D-38, same transaction
    });

    await test.step('11 · the tenant receives the bill on LINE', async () => {
      const text = await outbox.waitForText('ค่าใช้จ่ายประจำเดือน');
      for (const line of [
        `ห้อง ${ROOM}`,
        'ค่าน้ำ 10 หน่วย × 18 บาท = 180 บาท',
        'ค่าไฟ 70 หน่วย × 8 บาท = 560 บาท',
        'ค่าส่วนกลาง 30 ตร.ม. × 35 บาท = 1,050 บาท',
        'ยอดรวม 1,790 บาท',
        `กรุณาชำระภายในวันที่ ${thaiFullDate(expectedDueDate(PERIOD, 5))}`,
      ]) expect(text).toContain(line);
      expect(text).not.toMatch(/25\d\d/);                          // never Buddhist Era (D-03, R-11)

      const [row, ...rest] = await db.notifications(billId);
      expect(rest).toHaveLength(0);                                // one row per recipient, not per call
      expect(row).toMatchObject({ type: 'BILL', status: 'sent', attempt_count: 1 });
      // Test mode redirects the SEND; the row still records the intended recipient (D-34).
      expect(row.recipient_user_id).toMatch(/^Useed/);
    });

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ cash at the counter
    const payment = new PaymentPage(admin);
    const receipt = new ReceiptPage(admin);
    const periodTh = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('th-TH-u-ca-gregory-nu-latn',
      { month: 'long', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'Asia/Bangkok' })
      .format(new Date(PERIOD + '-15T12:00:00+07:00'));

    await test.step('12-13 · ยืนยันรับเงินสด takes exactly the bill total', async () => {
      await payment.goto();
      await payment.search(ROOM);
      await payment.selectBill(billId);
      await expect(payment.amountDue()).toHaveText('1,790.00');
      await payment.assertAmountIsNotAnInput();                    // D-24 — never an input
      await payment.confirmCash(ROOM, '1,790', periodTh);
    });

    await test.step('14 · the receipt, numbered from the run date', async () => {
      await expect(receipt.number()).toHaveText(receiptNo(852));   // REC-<YYYYMM>-000852
      await expect(admin.getByText('ใบเสร็จรับเงิน')).toBeVisible();
      await expect(receipt.amount('water')).toHaveText('180.00');
      await expect(receipt.amount('electric')).toHaveText('560.00');
      await expect(receipt.amount('common')).toHaveText('1,050.00');
      await expect(receipt.amount('total')).toHaveText('1,790.00');
      await expect(admin.getByText('หนึ่งพันเจ็ดร้อยเก้าสิบบาทถ้วน')).toBeVisible();
      await expect(admin.getByText('เงินสด')).toBeVisible();
      await expect(admin.getByText('สำเนา')).toHaveCount(0);       // original until the first print

      const paid = await db.one('SELECT status FROM bills WHERE id = ?', [billId]);
      expect(paid.status).toBe('Paid');
      const pay = await db.one(
        'SELECT amount, method, voided_at FROM payments WHERE bill_id = ?', [billId]);
      expect(pay).toMatchObject({ amount: '1790.00', method: 'cash', voided_at: null });
    });

    await test.step('15 · the tenant receives the confirmation', async () => {
      const text = await outbox.waitForText('ยืนยันการชำระเงิน');
      expect(text).toContain(`ห้อง ${ROOM}`);
      expect(text).toContain('1,790 บาท');
      expect(text).toContain(receiptNo(852));
      expect(text).toContain(thaiFullDate(todayBangkok()));

      const types = (await db.notifications(billId)).map((n) => n.type);
      expect(types).toEqual(['BILL', 'PAID']);
    });

    await test.step('16 · the Dashboard tiles reconcile and the report agrees', async () => {
      await admin.goto(`/dashboard?period=${PERIOD}`);
      await expect(admin.locator('[data-amount="common"]')).toHaveText('70,350.00');  // 67 x 30 x 35

      const money = (sel: string) => admin.locator(sel).innerText()
        .then((t) => Number(t.replace(/[^\d.]/g, '')));
      const billed = await money('[data-amount="billed"]');
      const collected = await money('[data-amount="collected"]');
      const outstanding = await money('[data-amount="outstanding"]');
      expect(collected).toBe(1790);                                 // the only Paid bill this period
      expect(Number((billed - collected).toFixed(2))).toBe(outstanding);   // FR-28 reconciliation

      const roomId = await db.roomId(ROOM);
      await admin.goto(`/reports/room-history?room_id=${roomId}`);
      const row = admin.locator(`tr[data-period="${PERIOD}"]`);
      await expect(row).toContainText('10');
      await expect(row).toContainText('70');
      await expect(row).toContainText('1,790.00');
      await expect(row.locator('[data-status]')).toHaveAttribute('data-status', 'Paid');
      await expect(row).toContainText(receiptNo(852));
    });

    await adminCtx.close();
  });
});
One extra hook the Dashboard step needs

Step 16 reads [data-amount="billed|collected|outstanding"]. Those are three more values of the same data-amount attribute already introduced in §2.10, rendered by the same money.ejs partial — not a new mechanism. The report row also uses data-period, which is the one place a period must be identified without reading the Thai month name; it is added to the same request.

7.9 API integration test — bill generation

Tier 2, node:test with built-in fetch against a server the api CI job starts before the suite runs (§2.11) — node --test boots no server of its own, unlike Playwright's webServer. test/helpers/db.js opens the pool, exposes rows/one/applyFixture, and runs the same one-line whitelist as global-setup.ts in its own setup, for the same reason (§2.5). The HTTP helper follows: the "15-line cookie helper" ADR A-9 promises, which in practice is thirty lines because it also has to carry the CSRF token.

// test/helpers/client.js — session cookie + CSRF, no supertest (ADR A-9)
const BASE = process.env.TEST_BASE_URL ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:3000';

export async function login(role) {
  const jar = { cookie: '', csrf: '' };
  const keep = (res) => {
    const set = res.headers.getSetCookie?.() ?? [];
    if (set.length) jar.cookie = set.map((c) => c.split(';')[0]).join('; ');
    return res;
  };

  // The token is issued on GET and read out of the form's hidden input.
  const form = await keep(await fetch(`${BASE}/login`, { headers: { cookie: jar.cookie } }));
  jar.csrf = /name="_csrf"\s+value="([^"]+)"/.exec(await form.text())[1];

  const password = role === 'admin'
    ? process.env.SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD
    : process.env.E2E_STAFF_PASSWORD ?? process.env.SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD;

  await keep(await fetch(`${BASE}/login`, {
    method: 'POST', redirect: 'manual',
    headers: { cookie: jar.cookie, 'content-type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
    body: new URLSearchParams({ username: role, password, _csrf: jar.csrf }),
  }));
  return jar;
}

/** JSON call. Returns { status, body } so a test can assert both halves of a failure. */
export async function api(jar, method, path, payload) {
  const res = await fetch(BASE + path, {
    method,
    headers: {
      cookie: jar.cookie,
      'content-type': 'application/json',
      'X-CSRF-Token': jar.csrf,
    },
    body: payload === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(payload),
  });
  return { status: res.status, body: await res.json().catch(() => null) };
}

// test/integration/bills.generate.test.js
import { test, before, beforeEach } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { login, api } from '../helpers/client.js';
import { db, applyFixture } from '../helpers/db.js';

const PERIOD = new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 3_600_000).toISOString().slice(0, 7);
let admin, roomId;

before(async () => {
  admin = await login('admin');
  roomId = (await db.one('SELECT id FROM rooms WHERE room_code = ?', ['205'])).id;
});

beforeEach(async () => {
  await applyFixture('reset-205.sql');
  await api(admin, 'POST', '/api/readings', {
    period: PERIOD, room_id: roomId,
    water:    { current_reading: 1260 },
    electric: { current_reading: 5350 },
  });
});

test('FR-14 FR-15 FR-16 FR-17 · D-16 D-18 · generation writes exactly three items and the §28 total', async () => {
  const run = await api(admin, 'POST', '/api/bills/generate', { period: PERIOD });
  assert.equal(run.status, 200);
  assert.equal(run.body.data.created, 1);

  const bill = await db.one(
    'SELECT id, status, total FROM bills WHERE room_id = ? AND period = ?', [roomId, PERIOD]);
  assert.equal(bill.status, 'Draft');
  assert.equal(bill.total, '1790.00');            // a string: mysql2 returns DECIMAL as text (D-16)

  const items = await db.rows(
    'SELECT item_type, quantity, unit_price, amount FROM bill_items WHERE bill_id = ? ORDER BY item_type',
    [bill.id]);
  assert.equal(items.length, 3);                  // never two, never four — uq_bill_items (ENG-2)
  assert.deepEqual(items, [
    { item_type: 'common',   quantity: '30.00', unit_price: '35.00', amount: '1050.00' },
    { item_type: 'electric', quantity: '70.00', unit_price: '8.00',  amount: '560.00'  },
    { item_type: 'water',    quantity: '10.00', unit_price: '18.00', amount: '180.00'  },
  ]);

  // The printed total is the sum of the printed lines. Satang arithmetic in the TEST only.
  const sum = items.reduce((acc, i) => acc + Math.round(Number(i.amount) * 100), 0);
  assert.equal(sum, Math.round(Number(bill.total) * 100));
});

test('FR-17 · D-18 · a second run creates nothing, recalculates Drafts and touches no approved bill', async () => {
  await api(admin, 'POST', '/api/bills/generate', { period: PERIOD });

  const approvedBefore = await db.rows(
    "SELECT id, total, status FROM bills WHERE period = ? AND status IN ('Unpaid','Paid') ORDER BY id", [PERIOD]);

  const again = await api(admin, 'POST', '/api/bills/generate', { period: PERIOD });
  assert.equal(again.status, 200);
  assert.equal(again.body.data.created, 0);       // uq_bills(room_id, period) is the idempotency key
  assert.equal(again.body.data.updated, 1);       // the one Draft is recalculated in place
  assert.equal(again.body.data.skipped, approvedBefore.length);
  assert.ok(Array.isArray(again.body.data.missing_rooms));

  const approvedAfter = await db.rows(
    "SELECT id, total, status FROM bills WHERE period = ? AND status IN ('Unpaid','Paid') ORDER BY id", [PERIOD]);
  assert.deepEqual(approvedAfter, approvedBefore); // an approved bill is never touched (D-18, D-22)

  const count = await db.one(
    'SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM bills WHERE room_id = ? AND period = ?', [roomId, PERIOD]);
  assert.equal(count.n, 1);
});

test('FR-12 · D-13 · a corrected reading recalculates the Draft bill in place on save', async () => {
  await api(admin, 'POST', '/api/bills/generate', { period: PERIOD });

  const saved = await api(admin, 'POST', '/api/readings', {
    period: PERIOD, room_id: roomId,
    water:    { current_reading: 1272 },   // 22 units instead of 10
    electric: { current_reading: 5350 },
  });
  assert.equal(saved.status, 200);
  assert.equal(saved.body.data.bill_recalculated, true);
  assert.equal(saved.body.data.water.units, 22);
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(saved.body).match(/\d+\.\d{2}/), 'the reading response carries no money');

  const bill = await db.one(
    'SELECT total FROM bills WHERE room_id = ? AND period = ?', [roomId, PERIOD]);
  assert.equal(bill.total, '2006.00');   // 22 x 18 = 396, + 560 + 1,050
});

test('FR-12 · D-13 · a current reading below the previous one is refused for every role', async () => {
  const res = await api(admin, 'POST', '/api/readings', {
    period: PERIOD, room_id: roomId,
    water: { current_reading: 1240 }, electric: { current_reading: 5350 },
  });
  assert.equal(res.status, 422);
  assert.equal(res.body.error.code, 'READING_BELOW_PREVIOUS');
  assert.match(res.body.error.message, /เลขมิเตอร์ปัจจุบันน้อยกว่าเดือนก่อน \(1,250\)/);
  assert.ok(res.body.error.fields, 'a 422 always carries fields keyed by input name');
  assert.equal(res.body.data, undefined, 'a response never carries both data and error');
});

7.10 Unit tests — the formulas, the rollover and rate resolution

Which half of a formula a unit test can prove

Each of the three formulas has two halves. The quantity half — current − previous, and the room's snapshotted area — is pure JavaScript and is unit-tested below. The money half — ROUND(quantity × unit_price, 2) — is executed by MySQL on DECIMAL (D-16, A-4), so no unit test can reach it; reimplementing it in JavaScript would be testing the test. It is proved instead by the DB-backed test above, which is the one that may never go red. Saying so plainly is better than a unit test that looks like it covers money and does not.

// test/unit/formulas.test.js — no database, runs in about a second
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { computeUnits, resolvePreviousReading, commonQuantity } from '../../services/billing.js';

test('FR-12 · D-14 · water units — PRD §28', () => {
  assert.equal(computeUnits({ previous_reading: 1250, current_reading: 1260 }), 10);
});

test('FR-13 · D-14 · electricity units — PRD §28', () => {
  assert.equal(computeUnits({ previous_reading: 5280, current_reading: 5350 }), 70);
});

test('FR-16 · D-09 · the common-area quantity is the BILL snapshot, not the live room', () => {
  // A bill approved at 30.00 must still print 30.00 after the room is changed to 32.00.
  assert.equal(commonQuantity({ room_area_sqm: '30.00', rooms: { area_sqm: '32.00' } }), '30.00');
});

test('FR-12 · D-13 · zero units is legal and is not an error', () => {
  assert.equal(computeUnits({ previous_reading: 1260, current_reading: 1260 }), 0);
});

test('FR-12 · D-13 · a current reading below the previous one throws, replacement box off', () => {
  assert.throws(
    () => computeUnits({ previous_reading: 1250, current_reading: 1240 }),
    /READING_BELOW_PREVIOUS/,
  );
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- rollover = meter replacement
// D-13: there is NO automatic rollover detection. A rolled dial is entered as a replacement,
// and the §28 arithmetic is deliberately unchanged by it — a replacement must not move money.

test('FR-33 · D-13 · a rolled dial entered as a replacement yields the same 10 units', () => {
  assert.equal(computeUnits({
    previous_reading: 1250, current_reading: 2,
    meter_replaced: true, old_final: 1258, new_start: 0,
    replace_reason: 'มิเตอร์น้ำชำรุด เปลี่ยนใหม่ต้นรอบ',
  }), 10);                                  // (1258 − 1250) + (2 − 0)
});

test('FR-33 · D-13 · the replacement branch suspends the below-previous block', () => {
  assert.equal(computeUnits({
    previous_reading: 1250, current_reading: 5,
    meter_replaced: true, old_final: 1250, new_start: 0,
  }), 5);                                   // current 5 < previous 1250, and that is correct here
});

test('FR-33 · D-13 · old_final below the previous reading is rejected', () => {
  assert.throws(() => computeUnits({
    previous_reading: 1250, current_reading: 2,
    meter_replaced: true, old_final: 1249, new_start: 0,
  }), /REPLACEMENT_INVALID/);
});

test('FR-33 · D-13 · a current reading below new_start is rejected', () => {
  assert.throws(() => computeUnits({
    previous_reading: 1250, current_reading: 5,
    meter_replaced: true, old_final: 1258, new_start: 10,
  }), /REPLACEMENT_INVALID/);
});

test('FR-33 · D-13 · units can never be negative, whichever branch runs', () => {
  for (const input of [
    { previous_reading: 0, current_reading: 0 },
    { previous_reading: 1250, current_reading: 1250, meter_replaced: true, old_final: 1250, new_start: 0 },
  ]) assert.ok(computeUnits(input) >= 0);   // mirrors CHECK (units >= 0)
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- previous-reading resolution
const rows = (...periods) => periods.map(([period, current_reading]) => ({ period, current_reading }));
const BASELINE = { baseline: 1250 };

test('FR-13 · D-06 · with no earlier row the room baseline applies, tagged "baseline"', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    resolvePreviousReading([], { period: '2026-09', ...BASELINE }),
    { previous_reading: 1250, previous_period: 'baseline' },
  );
});

test('FR-10 · D-14 · the greatest period strictly earlier wins, never the latest recorded_at', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    resolvePreviousReading(rows(['2026-06', 1180], ['2026-08', 1250], ['2026-07', 1210]),
      { period: '2026-09', ...BASELINE }),
    { previous_reading: 1250, previous_period: '2026-08' },
  );
});

test('FR-10 · D-14 · a skipped month resolves to the last month that exists, and does not block', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    resolvePreviousReading(rows(['2026-06', 1180], ['2026-07', 1210]), { period: '2026-09', ...BASELINE }),
    { previous_reading: 1210, previous_period: '2026-07' },
  );
});

test('FR-10 · D-12 · the row being written is excluded, so a double submit is harmless', () => {
  // The re-save of 2026-09 must not take its own 1,260 as its previous value.
  assert.deepEqual(
    resolvePreviousReading(rows(['2026-08', 1250], ['2026-09', 1260]), { period: '2026-09', ...BASELINE }),
    { previous_reading: 1250, previous_period: '2026-08' },
  );
});

test('FR-26 · D-14 · a reading taken while the room was vacant still counts', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    resolvePreviousReading(rows(['2026-08', 1250]), { period: '2026-09', ...BASELINE, vacantIn: ['2026-08'] }),
    { previous_reading: 1250, previous_period: '2026-08' },
  );
});

// test/unit/rates.test.js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { resolveRate } from '../../services/billing.js';

// rates is append-only and effective-dated: resolution takes the greatest effective_from
// that is on or before the FIRST DAY OF THE BILL'S PERIOD MONTH (D-17), never CURDATE().
const WATER = [
  { utility_type: 'water', unit_price: '16.00', effective_from: '2025-10-01' },
  { utility_type: 'water', unit_price: '18.00', effective_from: '2026-01-01' },
  { utility_type: 'water', unit_price: '19.50', effective_from: '2026-10-01' },  // future
];

test('FR-07 FR-08 · D-17 · the rate is keyed to the period, not to the moment generate was pressed', () => {
  assert.equal(resolveRate(WATER, '2026-09'), '18.00');
  assert.equal(resolveRate(WATER, '2025-12'), '16.00');
});

test('FR-08 · D-17 · a rate effective on the first day of the period month applies to it', () => {
  assert.equal(resolveRate(WATER, '2026-01'), '18.00');   // boundary: effective_from = 2026-01-01
});

test('FR-08 · D-17 · a future-dated rate never disturbs an earlier period', () => {
  assert.equal(resolveRate(WATER, '2026-09'), '18.00');   // 2026-10-01 > 2026-09-01, so 19.50 is ignored
  assert.equal(resolveRate(WATER, '2026-10'), '19.50');   // and it takes effect the moment its month opens
});

test('FR-08 · D-17 · a rate saved mid-period does not apply to that period', () => {
  const mid = [...WATER, { utility_type: 'water', unit_price: '20.00', effective_from: '2026-09-20' }];
  assert.equal(resolveRate(mid, '2026-09'), '18.00');     // 2026-09-20 > 2026-09-01
});

test('FR-08 · D-17 · no eligible row is a business rejection, never a silent 0', () => {
  assert.throws(() => resolveRate(WATER, '2025-01'), /RATE_NOT_FOUND/);
});

test('D-16 · the resolved price is returned as a string and never as a float', () => {
  assert.equal(typeof resolveRate(WATER, '2026-09'), 'string');
});

// test/unit/dueDate.test.js — the oracle from §2.6, asserted against the product's rule
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { computeDueDate } from '../../services/billing.js';

test('FR-18 · D-20 · the PRD §28 case — approval on the 1st, due day 5', () => {
  assert.equal(computeDueDate({ period: '2026-09', dueDay: 5, approvedOn: '2026-09-01' }), '2026-09-05');
});

test('FR-18 · D-20 · a late approval gets the three-day floor, not the due day', () => {
  assert.equal(computeDueDate({ period: '2026-09', dueDay: 5, approvedOn: '2026-09-10' }), '2026-09-13');
});

test('FR-18 · D-20 · the floor is never below approval + 3, even at the end of the month', () => {
  // The clamp applies to the day-of-month term only. See the S3 defect in §2.6.
  assert.equal(computeDueDate({ period: '2026-09', dueDay: 5, approvedOn: '2026-09-30' }), '2026-10-03');
});

test('FR-18 · D-20 · every bill in one batch approved on one day gets one due date', () => {
  const a = computeDueDate({ period: '2026-09', dueDay: 5, approvedOn: '2026-09-08' });
  const b = computeDueDate({ period: '2026-09', dueDay: 5, approvedOn: '2026-09-08' });
  assert.equal(a, b);
});

7.11 Assumptions recorded by this section

  1. Bill and receipt ids are never hard-coded. The design document's 1842 and 418 and the API specification's 118 and 96 are illustrative. Every test resolves an id by query — db.billId('205', PERIOD) — so a reseed cannot break a scenario.
  2. Only three things are hard-coded anywhere in the E2E tier: the room code 205, the §28 arithmetic (1,250 / 1,260 / 10 / 5,280 / 5,350 / 70 / 30.00 / 18.00 / 8.00 / 35.00 / 180.00 / 560.00 / 1,050.00 / 1,790.00), and the receipt sequence 852. Every date, period and receipt month is derived from the run date (D-44).
  3. data-amount extends to billed, collected and outstanding on the Dashboard, and data-period is added to report rows. Both are the same mechanism as §2.10, not new ones, as is data-previous on the reading card's read-only ครั้งก่อน value.
  4. The reading round's per-room save is asserted through the UI counter and the database, not through the JSON response, because the tenant-facing guarantee is that a lost connection loses at most the room being typed (D-15, D-40).
  5. Room codes 206–213 are used as scratch rooms by E2E-02, E2E-03 and E2E-05. They are ordinary seeded rooms with no special meaning; a fixture that ages or unlinks one is idempotent and is never applied to a demo database.
  6. E2E-02's ladder ceiling test needs a bill at day 22, which the due-dates.sql fixture provides on room 213 for that step only.
  7. Concurrency in E2E-07 step 6 is two fetch calls issued without awaiting the first, not a load generator. Two is enough: the guarantee under test is a unique key and a conditional UPDATE, and both fail at two if they fail at all.
  8. No scenario asserts a LINE delivery receipt. LINE reports a blocked recipient as success, so the word "blocked" appears in no badge, report or test case, and the gap is a listed limitation (D-05 item 9, D-30).
  9. Nothing in these seven scenarios touches a PRD §24 exclusion. Every payment is cash at the counter, the tenant's only client is LINE itself, and no test mentions rent, a lease, a gateway, a card, online banking, a slip, a QR code, an IoT meter or AI.

8. Traceability Matrix

Two matrices, both generated from the case tables above rather than maintained by hand: every functional requirement of engineering specification §1.5 — FR-01 … FR-49, the last of them added when it turned out that GET/POST /password was specified, designed nowhere and tested by three cases that traced to two requirements neither of which mentioned it — against the cases that discharge it, and every product decision D-01 … D-47 against the cases that exercise it. A requirement or decision with no case carries GAP and is a review finding, not a footnote. Case numbers are written against their prefix — TC-AUTH-034, 042 means TC-AUTH-034 and TC-AUTH-042, and an en dash is an inclusive run.

Both tables are the join of the Traces to cell of all 284 case rows in §3 – §6 with the scenario table of §8.3. They therefore say what each case declares it covers. The grouped roll-ups at the end of §3, §4, §5 and §6 say what their author intended a group of cases to cover, and are occasionally a little broader; where the two disagree, this section is authoritative.

Coverage at a glance

How this table is kept honest

Every case row in §3 – §6 ends in a Traces to cell, and every Playwright and node:test title begins with the same ids (§2.9). The matrix below is the join of those two facts. A case whose trace cell is empty has no authority and is deleted in review; a requirement whose row is empty is an untested requirement and blocks the milestone.

Counts exclude the E2E column, which is listed separately because a browser scenario covers a requirement end to end rather than case by case.

8.2 Product decisions → test cases

Requirements no browser scenario touches, and why that is correct

FR-03 … FR-09, FR-25, FR-26, FR-29, FR-30, FR-31, FR-33, FR-36, FR-41, FR-43, FR-44, FR-45 and FR-47 have no E2E row. Each is covered at tiers 1 and 2, where it belongs: a CRUD form, a rate lookup, a CSV byte-order mark and a SELECT 1 health check are all proved faster and more precisely without a browser (§2.2).

9.2 Decisions used

All forty-seven: D-01 … D-47. §8.2 lists each against the cases that exercise it, with zero gaps. The decisions this document leans on hardest, because a misreading of one would invalidate a whole group of cases, are:

DecisionWhy it shapes the plan
D-16 money in SQL on DECIMAL An amount can only be asserted against a database row or a rendered page, never against a JavaScript expression. Every money value is compared as a string — '1790.00', never 1790 — because mysql2 returns DECIMAL as text.
D-21 Overdue is derived No fake timer can move CURDATE(). Due-date tests move the data with one UPDATE relative to CURDATE() (§2.6).
D-26 duplicate prevention is a constraint The concurrency cases drive HTTP concurrently and assert what the database allowed, not what the button did.
D-34 the 500-message monthly allowance No automated tier ever holds a real channel token; announcements must be asserted as multicast, never as one push per tenant.
D-36 Staff never receive an amount Every Staff-forbidden route is asserted twice — 403 with the Thai page or FORBIDDEN_ROLE, and no digit-grouped amount anywhere in the response body.
D-44 every seed date is computed from the run date The PRD §28 calendar is a documentation value throughout; assertions are on arithmetic and on offsets from CURDATE().

9.3 Open contradictions raised by writing these tests

Each is a defect against a source document, not against the product, and each is recorded where the case that found it lives. None of them blocks a case: every one has a resolution QA has adopted in the meantime.

#ContradictionAdopted resolutionOwnerSeverity
1Room 205's tenant identity — engineering specification (สมชาย ใจดี, tenant 37, move-in 2025-10-01, LINE linked 2026-08-15) versus UI design (นางสาวอารยา ศรีสุวรรณ, move-in 2025-03-01, linked 2025-03-12), together with the design's /rooms/205, /bills/1842 and /receipts/1042 against the seeded ids 41 / 118 / 96Closed. The specification's seed row wins and the UI design has adopted it in full — name, both dates and all four route ids; อารยา ศรีสุวรรณ moves to room 202. No case asserts a literal name UI designS3
2The D-20 due-date clamp — read literally, a 30 September approval yields a due date one day after approval, below the three-day floor D-20 exists to guarantee The clamp binds the day-of-month term only, never the result of the MAX (§2.6)PM · ADRS3

No forced-redirect case may be written until a column exists PMS3
5Duplicate-username message — ชื่อผู้ใช้นี้ถูกใช้แล้ว versus ชื่อผู้ใช้นี้มีอยู่แล้วThe specification's catalogue string (TC-AUTH-037)UI designS3
